⸻

**1\. Purpose**

D1 menetapkan **Governance Operating Model** bagi PD-03 Governance &  
Compliance.

Jika:

- **Part A** menetapkan identity, strategic purpose, mandate, boundary,  
  objectives, dan strategic direction;
- **Part B** menetapkan organizational structure dan organizational  
  ownership;
- **Part C** menetapkan governance constitution, authority, decision,  
  delegation, accountability, review, escalation, cross-platform  
  governance, compliance, dan governance success;

maka:

**Part D menetapkan bagaimana PD-03 menjalankan governance dan  
compliance mandate tersebut sebagai operasi yang konsisten,  
traceable, controlled, evidence-based, dan continuously improving.**

D1 menjadi **constitutional operating parent** bagi D2–D10.

D1 tidak menggantikan Part C.

⸻

**2\. Operating Constitution**

Governance Operating Model merupakan kerangka yang mengubah:

Governance Mandate

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Work

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment / Review / Decision

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Artifact

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Outcome

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement

menjadi operating model yang dapat dijalankan secara konsisten.

Seluruh operating mechanism PD-03 harus:

- berada dalam mandate PD-03;
- berada dalam organizational boundary PD-03;
- mengikuti authority yang telah ditetapkan;
- memiliki operational ownership;
- menghasilkan evidence yang dapat ditelusuri;
- menjaga separation of concern;
- mendukung compliance;
- dapat direview;
- dapat dikendalikan;
- dapat ditingkatkan.

⸻

**3\. Operating Domain**

Operating Model PD-03 beroperasi pada domain:

Governance & Compliance

Core responsibility yang telah ditetapkan untuk PD-03 meliputi:

Policy

Control

Certification

Compliance

Baseline juga menetapkan Policy dan Standards sebagai primary outputs  
PD-03 serta Governance Baseline sebagai canonical reference.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

D1 mengoperasikan responsibility tersebut.

D1 **tidak memperluas domain PD-03 menjadi general enterprise  
operations**.

⸻

**4\. Operating Purpose**

Governance Operating Model memiliki tujuan:

**GOM-01 — Governance Execution Consistency**

Memastikan governance mandate dijalankan secara konsisten.

⸻

**GOM-02 — Governance Requirement Alignment**

Memastikan operating activity tetap aligned dengan:

- Governance Baseline;
- applicable policy;
- applicable standards;
- governance controls;
- certification requirements;
- compliance requirements.

⸻

**GOM-03 — Governance Traceability**

Memastikan governance work dapat ditelusuri dari requirement hingga  
outcome.

Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Activity

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Decision / Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Action

&nbsp; ↓

Outcome

⸻

**GOM-04 — Governance Assurance**

Memastikan governance activity menghasilkan evidence yang memadai untuk  
assessment, review, compliance, dan assurance.

⸻

**GOM-05 — Controlled Governance Operation**

Memastikan governance operation berjalan dalam authority dan boundary  
yang telah ditetapkan.

⸻

**GOM-06 — Continuous Governance Improvement**

Memastikan operating model dapat diperbaiki berdasarkan:

- findings;
- evidence;
- review results;
- compliance results;
- recurring governance issues;
- lessons learned.

⸻

**5\. Operating Philosophy**

**GOP-01 — Governance Before Uncontrolled Execution**

Governance operation harus berjalan berdasarkan governance framework  
yang telah ditetapkan.

Operating convenience tidak boleh digunakan untuk bypass applicable  
governance requirement.

⸻

**GOP-02 — Evidence Before Assertion**

Material governance conclusion harus memiliki evidence yang sesuai.

Claim

&nbsp;↓

Evidence

&nbsp;↓

Assessment

&nbsp;↓

Conclusion

⸻

**GOP-03 — Authority Before Action**

Governance activity yang memerlukan authority tertentu harus terlebih  
dahulu berada pada authority yang applicable.

D1 tidak menciptakan authority baru.

⸻

**GOP-04 — Domain Ownership Preservation**

PD-03 menjalankan governance responsibility tanpa mengambil operational  
execution ownership dari Platform lain.

Model canonical:

Governance Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Domain Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Execution

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03 Governance / Compliance Assessment

Prinsip ownership ini konsisten dengan boundary PD-03 yang menetapkan  
bahwa governance/compliance tidak otomatis menjadi execution owner  
Platform lain.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

**GOP-05 — Separation of Concern**

Governance operation harus tetap dibedakan dari:

- operational execution;
- architecture execution;
- security execution;
- engineering execution;
- performance measurement.

⸻

**GOP-06 — Traceability by Design**

Governance work harus dirancang agar decision, evidence, finding, action,  
dan outcome dapat direkonstruksi.

⸻

**GOP-07 — Controlled Improvement**

Perubahan operating model harus didasarkan pada evidence dan mengikuti  
applicable change authority.

⸻

**6\. Operating Principles**

**GOPR-01 — Explicit Ownership**

Setiap material governance activity memiliki operational owner.

Operational ownership tidak otomatis sama dengan:

- organizational ownership;
- governance authority;
- accountability;
- execution ownership.

⸻

**GOPR-02 — Requirement Traceability**

Setiap material governance activity harus dapat dikaitkan dengan  
requirement yang berlaku.

⸻

**GOPR-03 — Evidence Integrity**

Evidence harus:

- relevan;
- dapat ditelusuri;
- memiliki provenance;
- dipelihara sesuai applicable record requirement.

Historical evidence tidak boleh diubah hanya untuk memperbaiki  
governance outcome.

⸻

**GOPR-04 — Controlled Flexibility**

Operating flexibility diperbolehkan selama:

- tidak melanggar Governance Architecture;
- tidak melampaui authority;
- tidak menghapus required evidence;
- tidak mengubah ownership secara tidak sah.

⸻

**GOPR-05 — Reviewability**

Material governance work harus dapat direview oleh applicable mechanism.

⸻

**GOPR-06 — Improvement Orientation**

Operational finding tidak berhenti pada closure; material learning harus  
dapat menjadi input improvement.

⸻

D1 menggunakan layered operating model:

Governance Mandate

Setiap layer memiliki concern berbeda.

D1 tidak menetapkan detail mekanisme masing-masing layer; detail tersebut  
diturunkan ke D2–D10.

⸻

**8\. Governance Work Model**

Unit operasi utama PD-03 adalah **Governance Work**.

Governance Work dapat mencakup:

- policy development;
- governance standards work;
- control definition or assessment;
- certification-related governance work;
- compliance assessment;
- governance review preparation;
- governance evidence management;
- governance finding management;
- corrective-action coordination;
- governance improvement analysis.

Daftar tersebut menunjukkan **kelas pekerjaan**, bukan penetapan  
subdivision atau role baru.

Detail classification dan workflow ditetapkan pada child sections.

⸻

**9\. Governance Work Lifecycle**

Secara constitutional, governance work mengikuti lifecycle:

Identify

&nbsp; ↓

Classify

&nbsp; ↓

Scope

&nbsp; ↓

Plan

&nbsp; ↓

Execute

&nbsp; ↓

Assess / Review

&nbsp; ↓

Decide / Disposition

&nbsp; ↓

Record

&nbsp; ↓

Close

&nbsp; ↓

Learn

&nbsp; ↓

Improve

Tidak setiap governance work harus memiliki tingkat formalitas yang sama.

**Materiality menentukan depth of treatment.**

⸻

**10\. Policy Operating Boundary**

PD-03 memiliki responsibility terhadap **Governance Policy**.

Karena itu operating model dapat menjalankan:

- policy development;
- policy maintenance;
- policy review;
- policy alignment;
- policy compliance assessment.

Namun:

Governance Policy

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03

Technical / Domain Policy

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Domain Owner

PD-03 tidak menjadi automatic owner untuk seluruh policy AIOS hanya  
karena policy tersebut memiliki governance implication.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

**11\. Control Operating Boundary**

PD-03 memiliki governance responsibility terhadap **Control**.

Operating model PD-03 dapat:

- define governance control requirements;
- coordinate control assessment;
- evaluate control conformance;
- manage governance control evidence;
- identify control findings;
- coordinate corrective action.

Namun:

**Governance control ownership ≠ implementation ownership.**

Control implementation tetap berada pada applicable domain owner.

⸻

**12\. Certification Operating Boundary**

PD-03 memiliki organizational responsibility terhadap **Certification**.

Operating model certification harus mempertahankan:

Certification

&nbsp; ≠

Implementation

PD-03 dapat menjalankan certification governance process.

Subject yang menjalankan domain implementation tetap mempertahankan  
execution ownership.

⸻

Compliance operation mengikuti:

Corrective Action

&nbsp; ↓

Validation

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement

**14\. Governance Artifact Operations**

Material governance operation harus menghasilkan artifact yang sesuai  
dengan activity.

Potential artifact classes:

- policy;
- standard;
- governance record;
- assessment record;
- certification record;
- compliance evidence;
- finding;
- corrective-action record;
- validation record;
- governance improvement record.

Nama repository, register ID, atau exact artifact schema yang belum  
ditetapkan source **tidak boleh dibuat sebagai canonical fact**.

⸻

**15\. Governance Decision Boundary**

D1 membedakan:

Governance Operation

&nbsp; ≠

Governance Authority

Operating activity dapat:

- prepare;
- analyze;
- coordinate;
- document;
- assess;
- recommend.

Decision tetap mengikuti authority pada Part C.

Dengan demikian:

**Operating Model does not create decision authority.**

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Escalation Governance

**18\. Cross-Platform Operating Interface**

PD-03 harus berinteraksi dengan Platform lain tanpa menghapus ownership  
mereka.

PD-03 Governance Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Platform

&nbsp; ↓

Implementation / Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Disposition

Coordination bukan ownership transfer.

Model ini menjaga prinsip:

**Governance coordinates and assures; domain owners execute within  
their applicable authority.**

selama penyesuaian tidak mengubah:

**31\. Child Boundary Rules**

**D2 — Executive Governance Interface**

Mengatur bagaimana PD-03 berinteraksi dengan Executive Office.

Tidak mengambil executive authority.

⸻

**D3 — Platform Governance Coordination**

Mengatur koordinasi governance dengan Platform lain.

Tidak mengambil domain execution ownership.

⸻

**D4 — Governance Decision Operations**

Mengatur operational preparation dan flow governance decision.

Tidak mengubah authority C2/C5.

⸻

**D5 — Governance Delegation Operations**

Mengatur operational handling delegation.

Tidak menciptakan delegation authority baru.

⸻

**D6 — Governance Operating Rhythm**

Mengatur timing dan cadence governance operation.

⸻

**D7 — Governance Collaboration Architecture**

Mengatur collaboration mechanism dalam governance work.

⸻

**D8 — Governance Operational Control**

Mengatur bagaimana governance operation tetap controlled dan traceable.

⸻

**D9 — Governance Operating Improvement**

Mengatur bagaimana operating model diperbaiki berdasarkan evidence.

⸻

**D10 — Governance Operating Success**

Menetapkan kapan Governance Operating Model dianggap berhasil.

**32\. Anti-Patterns**

D1 melarang:

**AP-01 — Governance Takeover**

PD-03 mengambil execution ownership Platform lain.

**AP-02 — Authority Manufacture**

Operating process menciptakan authority baru.

**AP-03 — Compliance as Execution**

Compliance activity berubah menjadi operational execution.

**AP-04 — Policy Expansion**

PD-03 menganggap seluruh policy AIOS sebagai automatic ownership.

**AP-05 — Evidence Without Provenance**

Governance conclusion dibuat dari evidence yang tidak dapat ditelusuri.

**AP-06 — Governance Bypass**

Operating convenience digunakan untuk melewati governance requirement.

**AP-07 — Historical Rewriting**

Historical governance evidence diubah untuk menghasilkan outcome yang  
lebih baik.

**AP-08 — Performance Duplication**

D1 membuat KPI atau performance framework yang seharusnya berada pada  
Part E.

**AP-09 — Governance Constitution Duplication**

D1 mengulang atau mengubah authority/constitution yang sudah ditetapkan  
Part C.

⸻

**33\. Governance Operating Success Conditions**

D1 dianggap structurally successful apabila:

1. governance work memiliki operating basis;
2. Policy, Control, Certification, dan Compliance dapat dioperasikan  
   dalam boundary PD-03;
3. governance work dapat ditelusuri;
4. evidence dapat dipelihara;
5. operational ownership jelas;
6. governance authority tidak berubah;
7. cross-platform operation tidak mengambil ownership;
8. review dan escalation tetap mengikuti Part C;
9. governance operation dapat menghasilkan improvement input;
10. D2–D10 memiliki boundary yang jelas;
11. tidak terdapat material overlap dengan Part B, C, E, atau F.

⸻

**34\. Canonical Governance Operating Model Statement**

**PD-03 Governance & Compliance operates through a governed,  
evidence-based, traceable, and continuously improving operating model  
that converts governance mandate into policy, control, certification,  
compliance, assessment, evidence, disposition, and improvement while  
preserving authority boundaries, domain ownership, Platform autonomy,  
and the separation between governance assurance and operational  
execution.**

Versi Bahasa Indonesia:

**PD-03 Governance & Compliance beroperasi melalui operating model yang  
governed, evidence-based, traceable, dan terus diperbaiki untuk  
menerjemahkan governance mandate menjadi policy, control,  
certification, compliance, assessment, evidence, disposition, dan  
improvement dengan tetap menjaga batas authority, domain ownership,  
autonomy Platform, serta pemisahan antara governance assurance dan  
operational execution.**

**35\. Source-Fidelity Qualification**

Status D1 adalah:

**BOUNDED CANONICAL SYNTHESIS**

**Source-supported**

- PD-03 = Governance & Compliance.
- Governance domain.
- Policy responsibility.
- Control responsibility.
- Certification responsibility.
- Compliance responsibility.
- Governance Standards / Policy outputs.
- Governance Baseline relationship.
- Domain ownership preservation.
- Governance/compliance versus operational execution boundary.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

**Reference-inherited**

- D1 sebagai constitutional operating parent.
- Operating purpose/principles pattern.
- Governance work lifecycle.
- Operating traceability model.
- Child D2–D10 operating decomposition.
- Operation → evidence → improvement model.

**Source gap**

Belum ditemukan secara eksplisit untuk PD-03:

- exact D1 baseline text;
- exact operating role model;
- exact operating cadence;
- exact materiality taxonomy;
- exact operating register;
- exact workflow schema;
- exact operating KPI;
- exact maturity taxonomy.

**Tidak satu pun source gap tersebut diubah menjadi fact canonical.**

Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance

Part D — Governance Operations

D2 — Executive Governance Interface

Section ID: D2  
Section Name: Executive Governance Interface  
Platform ID: PD-03  
Official Name: Governance & Compliance  
Volume: Volume 3  
Part: Part D — Governance Operations  
Parent: D1 — Governance Operating Model  
Predecessor: D1 — Governance Operating Model

Status: FROZEN — SOURCE-BOUNDED  
Gold Standard Review: PASS WITH CONTROLLED SOURCE GAP  
Freeze Decision: APPROVED

⸻

1\. Purpose

D2 menetapkan bagaimana PD-03 Governance & Compliance berinteraksi  
secara operasional dengan PD-01 Executive Office.

D2 menyediakan interface untuk:

• strategic governance context;

• governance reporting;

• governance assessment;

• material governance issue;

• executive review;

• escalation;

• governance recommendation;

• authorized executive disposition;

• governance improvement feedback.

D2 tidak mengubah authority model yang telah ditetapkan pada Part C.

⸻

2\. Executive Interface Constitution

Prinsip utama:

PD-03 menerima executive direction dan enterprise governance context,  
menginterpretasikannya dalam domain Governance & Compliance, kemudian  
mengembalikan governance assessment, findings, recommendations, dan  
material governance matters kepada Executive Office melalui applicable  
authority mechanism.

Model:

Executive Direction / Context

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Interpretation

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Work

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment / Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Result

&nbsp; ↓

Executive Interface

⸻

3\. Interface Boundary

D2 mencakup hubungan:

PD-01 Executive Office

&nbsp; ↕

PD-03 Governance & Compliance

D2 tidak menetapkan hubungan internal:

• Executive Office → Executive Sub Division;

• PD-03 → Sub Division;

• PD-03 → Team;

• Team → Role Group.

Struktur organisasi berada pada Part B.

⸻

4\. Executive Office Role

Dalam interface D2, Executive Office berfungsi sebagai enterprise  
strategic and executive authority interface.

Executive Office dapat menyediakan:

• strategic direction;

• enterprise priorities;

• executive decisions;

• material governance disposition;

• enterprise-level conflict resolution;

• executive review outcome.

Reference organizational model menetapkan Executive Office sebagai  
enterprise-level authority, sedangkan Platform Division mempertahankan  
domain ownership masing-masing.

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

⸻

5\. PD-03 Role

PD-03 menyediakan governance expertise dan governance operating  
capability dalam domainnya.

PD-03 dapat:

• menerjemahkan executive direction menjadi governance context;

• mengembangkan atau memelihara governance policy;

• mengoperasikan governance controls;

• menjalankan certification governance;

• menjalankan compliance activity;

• menyediakan governance assessment;

• menyediakan governance evidence;

• mengidentifikasi governance finding;

• menyampaikan recommendation;

• melakukan governance improvement.

PD-03 tidak mengambil alih enterprise strategic direction.

⸻

6\. Interface Principle — Direction, Not Execution

Executive Office memberikan arah dan authority.

PD-03 mengoperasikan governance domain.

Executive Office

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; │ Direction / Decision

&nbsp; ▼

PD-03

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; │ Governance Operation

&nbsp; ▼

Governance Outcome

Hubungan tersebut tidak berarti Executive Office menjalankan  
operational governance work secara langsung.

Sebaliknya, PD-03 tidak memperoleh executive authority hanya karena  
menyediakan governance assessment.

⸻

7\. Interface Principle — Dependency ≠ Subordination

Interface D2 tidak menciptakan hierarchy baru.

Interface

&nbsp; ≠

Subordination

PD-03 tetap merupakan Platform Division dengan Governance Authority.

Executive Office tetap merupakan enterprise-level executive authority.

Hubungan keduanya ditentukan oleh authority model, bukan oleh frekuensi  
interaksi.

⸻

8\. Strategic Direction Intake

PD-03 dapat menerima executive direction yang memiliki governance  
implication.

Contoh:

• perubahan strategic objective;

• perubahan governance priority;

• enterprise-level governance concern;

• material organizational change;

• material policy requirement;

• executive governance decision.

Alur:

Executive Direction

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Impact Analysis

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03 Governance Work

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Response

Executive direction tidak otomatis menjadi policy.

Policy tetap harus melalui applicable governance mechanism.

⸻

9\. Governance Interpretation

PD-03 bertugas menerjemahkan executive context ke dalam domain  
Governance & Compliance.

Executive Context

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Interpretation

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Mechanism

Interpretation tidak boleh:

• mengubah strategic intent;

• menciptakan authority baru;

• memperluas PD-03 ownership;

• bypass governance process.

⸻

10\. Governance Briefing

PD-03 dapat menyediakan governance briefing kepada Executive Office  
untuk:

• material governance condition;

• compliance status;

• governance risk;

• unresolved finding;

• policy issue;

• control issue;

• certification concern;

• governance improvement need.

Briefing harus membedakan:

Fact

Evidence

Assessment

Recommendation

Decision

Recommendation tidak boleh dipresentasikan sebagai executive decision.

⸻

11\. Governance Reporting

Governance reporting kepada Executive Office harus menyediakan  
informasi yang relevan terhadap executive decision.

Minimum conceptual content:

• governance condition;

• material issue;

• impact;

• evidence;

• applicable requirement;

• current disposition;

• recommendation;

• unresolved dependency;

• required decision, jika ada.

Exact PD-03 executive reporting schema belum ditemukan dalam source.

Karena itu schema tersebut tidak dibekukan sebagai mandatory template.

⸻

12\. Executive Review Interface

D2 menyediakan operational interface terhadap executive review.

Model:

Governance Matter

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03 Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Review

&nbsp; ↓

Executive Review — if material/applicable

&nbsp; ↓

Executive Disposition

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03 Operational Follow-through

D2 tidak menggantikan C6 — Architecture/Executive Review Governance  
atau governance review mechanism yang sudah ditetapkan pada Part C.

⸻

13\. Materiality Boundary

Tidak seluruh governance activity harus dibawa ke Executive Office.

Executive interface digunakan ketika matter:

• memiliki enterprise-level consequence;

• berada di luar available Platform authority;

• memiliki material cross-platform impact;

• membutuhkan executive decision;

• membutuhkan executive disposition;

• memiliki unresolved governance conflict yang memerlukan authority  
lebih tinggi.

Exact materiality threshold PD-03 belum ditemukan.

Jangan membekukan numerical threshold tanpa source.

⸻

14\. Executive Decision Interface

Jika governance matter membutuhkan executive decision:

PD-03

Analysis

&nbsp; ↓

Recommendation

&nbsp; ↓

Executive Decision Authority

&nbsp; ↓

Decision

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03 Implementation / Governance Follow-through

PD-03 menyediakan governance context.

Executive Office menggunakan applicable executive authority untuk  
decision.

⸻

15\. Decision vs Recommendation

D2 harus menjaga separation:

Governance Recommendation

&nbsp; ≠

Executive Decision

PD-03 dapat:

• recommend;

• assess;

• provide options;

• provide impact analysis.

PD-03 tidak boleh menyatakan recommendation sebagai final executive  
decision kecuali authority memang telah didelegasikan secara eksplisit.

⸻

16\. Governance Escalation Interface

Jika governance matter tidak dapat diselesaikan pada applicable  
authority:

Governance Issue

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03 Resolution Attempt

&nbsp; ↓

Unresolved

&nbsp; ↓

C7 Escalation Governance

&nbsp; ↓

Executive Authority, if applicable

&nbsp; ↓

Disposition

D2 menyediakan interface.

C7 tetap menjadi constitutional escalation mechanism.

⸻

17\. Executive Conflict Resolution

Apabila governance issue memiliki conflict antar Platform atau authority  
boundary:

Issue

&nbsp;↓

Governance Analysis

&nbsp;↓

Cross-Platform Assessment

&nbsp;↓

Applicable Escalation

&nbsp;↓

Executive Resolution if Required

Executive resolution tidak secara otomatis mengubah domain ownership.

⸻

18\. Executive Governance Evidence

PD-03 harus menyediakan evidence yang cukup ketika executive interface  
digunakan.

Evidence dapat mencakup:

• applicable policy;

• governance standard;

• control evidence;

• compliance assessment;

• governance finding;

• certification evidence;

• cross-platform governance record;

• prior decision;

• impact analysis.

Evidence harus mempertahankan provenance dan historical integrity.

⸻

Canonical information flow:

Executive Office

&nbsp; │ Direction / Context

&nbsp; ▼

20\. Executive Feedback Loop

D2 membentuk feedback loop:

Executive Direction

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Operation

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Outcome

&nbsp; ↓

Executive Review

&nbsp; ↓

Executive Decision / Feedback

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Improvement

&nbsp; ↓

Next Governance Cycle

Feedback tidak boleh digunakan untuk bypass governance architecture.

⸻

21\. Governance Policy Interface

Executive Office dapat memberikan strategic requirement atau executive  
direction yang memiliki policy implication.

Namun:

Executive Direction

&nbsp; ↓

Policy Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Policy Process

Executive direction tidak otomatis menjadi final policy artifact.

Policy tetap mengikuti Governance Architecture dan applicable  
governance process.

⸻

22\. Governance Control Interface

Executive Office dapat meminta visibility terhadap material governance  
control condition.

PD-03 menyediakan:

• control assessment;

• control evidence;

• material control finding;

• control improvement recommendation.

PD-03 tidak mengambil alih technical control implementation Platform  
lain.

⸻

23\. Certification Interface

Material certification status atau certification issue dapat  
dikomunikasikan kepada Executive Office.

Model:

Certification Activity

&nbsp; ↓

Certification Result

&nbsp; ↓

Materiality Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Executive Visibility if Required

Executive visibility tidak berarti Executive Office menjadi  
certification operator.

⸻

24\. Compliance Interface

D2 menghubungkan hasil C9 dengan executive governance visibility.

C9

Governance Compliance

&nbsp; ↓

Compliance Result

&nbsp; ↓

Materiality

&nbsp; ↓

Executive Governance Interface

&nbsp; ↓

Executive Awareness / Decision

C9 tetap menjadi compliance mechanism.

D2 hanya menyediakan interface.

⸻

25\. Governance Risk Interface

Governance risk yang material dapat dikomunikasikan kepada Executive  
Office apabila:

• memiliki enterprise implication;

• tidak dapat diselesaikan pada Platform level;

• membutuhkan executive decision;

• memiliki significant cross-platform consequence.

Exact risk taxonomy belum ditetapkan dalam D2 source.

⸻

26\. Cross-Platform Governance Interface

PD-03 dapat menyampaikan kepada Executive Office apabila governance  
issue memiliki:

• multiple Platform impact;

• ownership conflict;

• authority conflict;

• policy conflict;

• unresolved dependency;

• material compliance implication.

Model:

PD-03

Governance Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Cross-Platform Impact

&nbsp; ↓

Executive Visibility / Escalation

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Disposition

⸻

27\. Ownership Preservation

D2 harus selalu mempertahankan:

Executive Authority

&nbsp; ≠

PD-03 Domain Ownership

dan:

PD-03 Governance Authority

&nbsp; ≠

Other Platform Execution Ownership

Baseline PD-03 secara eksplisit menetapkan bahwa governance relationship  
tidak memindahkan ownership Platform lain.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

28\. No Governance Takeover

Executive interface tidak boleh digunakan untuk:

• mengambil alih domain Platform;

• bypass Architecture Authority;

• bypass Security Authority;

• bypass Quality Authority;

• bypass Runtime Authority;

• bypass applicable governance delegation;

• mengubah organizational ownership secara informal.

⸻

29\. Executive Interface Records

Material executive governance interaction harus dapat ditelusuri.

Potential record classes:

• Executive Governance Brief;

• Governance Assessment;

• Governance Recommendation;

• Executive Decision;

• Executive Disposition;

• Governance Follow-up;

• Escalation Record.

Nama resmi repository/register belum ditemukan sebagai PD-03-specific  
source.

Karena itu daftar tersebut merupakan artifact classes, bukan  
repository mandate.

⸻

30\. Interface Control

Setiap material interface harus memiliki:

• identifiable subject;

• purpose;

• authority context;

• evidence basis;

• responsible owner;

• outcome;

• traceability.

Interface tidak harus berarti meeting atau synchronous communication.

Interface dapat berupa:

• formal report;

• decision package;

• assessment;

• escalation;

• governance record;

• executive review.

⸻

31\. Executive Interface Materiality

D2 menggunakan prinsip:

Material Governance Matter

&nbsp; ↓

Executive Interface

Sedangkan:

Routine Governance Work

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03 Operating Model

Routine governance work tidak perlu diekskalasikan ke Executive Office  
kecuali terdapat applicable trigger.

⸻

32\. Executive Interface Independence

PD-03 tetap harus mempertahankan integrity assessment ketika berinteraksi  
dengan Executive Office.

Executive expectation tidak boleh mengubah evidence-based assessment  
menjadi predetermined conclusion.

Model:

Executive Interest

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment Context

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Conclusion

bukan:

Executive Preference

&nbsp; ↓

Forced Governance Conclusion

33\. Historical Integrity

Executive interface tidak memberikan authority untuk mengubah  
historical governance evidence.

Jika terdapat correction:

Original Record

&nbsp; ↓

Controlled Correction

&nbsp; ↓

Correction Trace

&nbsp; ↓

Current Record

Historical state harus tetap dapat direkonstruksi.

36\. Relationship to Part C

D2 mengoperasikan authority yang sudah ditetapkan pada:

• C2 — Decision Governance;

• C3 — Delegation Governance;

• C4 — Accountability Governance;

• C5 — Authority Governance;

• C6 — Review Governance;

• C7 — Escalation Governance;

• C8 — Cross-Platform Governance;

• C9 — Governance Compliance;

• C10 — Governance Success.

D2 tidak membuat constitutional substitute.

⸻

37\. Relationship to Part E

D2 menghasilkan operational evidence yang dapat digunakan oleh  
Performance Architecture.

Tetapi:

Executive Governance Interface ≠ Performance Governance.

D2 tidak menetapkan:

• KPI;

• performance threshold;

• performance score;

• performance maturity level.

⸻

38\. Anti-Patterns

D2 melarang:

AP-01 — Executive Bypass

Melewati applicable governance process hanya karena matter bersifat  
executive.

AP-02 — Executive Takeover

Executive interface digunakan untuk mengambil alih domain execution  
Platform.

AP-03 — Recommendation as Decision

Governance recommendation diperlakukan sebagai executive decision.

AP-04 — Informal Authority Creation

Interaksi executive menciptakan authority yang tidak tercatat pada  
authority model.

AP-05 — Reporting as Ownership

Menerima report dari Platform tidak berarti PD-03 atau Executive Office  
memiliki domain tersebut.

AP-06 — Forced Compliance Conclusion

Executive expectation digunakan untuk memaksa compliance conclusion.

AP-07 — Historical Rewriting

Executive interface digunakan untuk mengubah historical evidence.

AP-08 — Routine Escalation

Seluruh operational matter dinaikkan ke Executive Office tanpa  
materiality basis.

⸻

39\. Success Criteria

D2 dianggap berhasil apabila:

0\. executive direction dapat diterima oleh PD-03 secara terstruktur;

0\. governance interpretation dapat dilakukan tanpa mengubah strategic  
intent;

0\. material governance result dapat disampaikan kepada Executive Office;

0\. recommendation dan decision tetap terpisah;

0\. executive decision dapat dikembalikan ke PD-03 sebagai authorized  
disposition;

0\. escalation tetap mengikuti C7;

0\. governance review tetap mengikuti applicable review authority;

0\. PD-03 tidak kehilangan Governance Authority;

0\. Platform lain tidak kehilangan domain ownership;

0\. executive interface tidak menciptakan hierarchy baru;

0\. evidence dan historical records tetap traceable;

0\. governance feedback dapat dikembalikan ke operating model;

0\. D3 dapat menangani coordination tanpa menduplikasi D2.

⸻

40\. Canonical Interface Statement

PD-03 Governance & Compliance interfaces with the Executive Office  
through a controlled governance channel in which executive direction  
and enterprise context are translated into applicable governance work,  
while governance assessment, evidence, material findings, recommendations,  
and required dispositions are returned to the Executive Office through  
the applicable authority, review, and escalation mechanisms. The  
interface does not create subordination, transfer Platform ownership,  
or manufacture authority.

Versi Indonesia:

PD-03 Governance & Compliance berinterface dengan Executive Office  
melalui kanal governance yang terkontrol, di mana executive direction  
dan enterprise context diterjemahkan menjadi governance work yang  
applicable, sementara governance assessment, evidence, material  
findings, recommendations, dan disposition yang diperlukan  
dikembalikan kepada Executive Office melalui authority, review, dan  
escalation mechanism yang berlaku. Interface ini tidak menciptakan  
subordination, memindahkan ownership Platform, ataupun menciptakan  
authority baru.

⸻

41\. Source-Fidelity Qualification

SOURCE-SUPPORTED

• PD-03 = Governance & Compliance.

• PD-03 memiliki Governance Authority.

• Policy, Control, Certification, dan Compliance merupakan governance  
responsibility.

• Executive Office memiliki enterprise-level executive authority.

• Platform mempertahankan domain ownership.

• Governance relationship tidak otomatis menjadi organizational control.

• Dependency tidak sama dengan subordination.

REFERENCE-ADAPTED

• Executive Office → Platform strategic interface.

• Strategic Direction / Enterprise Context sebagai input.

• Executive governance feedback loop.

• Governance reporting interface.

• Executive disposition pattern.

Pola interface Executive Office → Platform secara eksplisit terlihat pada  
reference PD-02.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

SOURCE GAP

Belum ditemukan secara literal:

• PD-03 D2 definition;

• exact executive reporting format;

• exact executive review cadence;

• exact materiality threshold;

• exact executive approval matrix khusus PD-03;

• exact interface artifact/register;

• exact escalation trigger khusus D2.

Semua tetap SOURCE GAP, bukan canonical fact.

⸻

**6\. Interface Principle — Direction, Not Execution**

Executive Office memberikan arah dan authority.

PD-03 mengoperasikan governance domain.

Executive Office

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; │ Direction / Decision

&nbsp; ▼

PD-03

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; │ Governance Operation

&nbsp; ▼

Governance Outcome

Hubungan tersebut tidak berarti Executive Office menjalankan  
operational governance work secara langsung.

Sebaliknya, PD-03 tidak memperoleh executive authority hanya karena  
menyediakan governance assessment.

⸻

**7\. Interface Principle — Dependency ≠ Subordination**

Interface D2 tidak menciptakan hierarchy baru.

Interface

&nbsp; ≠

Subordination

PD-03 tetap merupakan Platform Division dengan Governance Authority.

Executive Office tetap merupakan enterprise-level executive authority.

Hubungan keduanya ditentukan oleh authority model, bukan oleh frekuensi  
interaksi.

⸻

**8\. Strategic Direction Intake**

PD-03 dapat menerima executive direction yang memiliki governance  
implication.

Contoh:

- perubahan strategic objective;
- perubahan governance priority;
- enterprise-level governance concern;
- material organizational change;
- material policy requirement;
- executive governance decision.

Alur:

Executive Direction

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Impact Analysis

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03 Governance Work

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Response

Executive direction tidak otomatis menjadi policy.

Policy tetap harus melalui applicable governance mechanism.

⸻

**9\. Governance Interpretation**

PD-03 bertugas menerjemahkan executive context ke dalam domain  
Governance & Compliance.

Executive Context

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Interpretation

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Mechanism

Interpretation tidak boleh:

- mengubah strategic intent;
- menciptakan authority baru;
- memperluas PD-03 ownership;
- bypass governance process.

⸻

**10\. Governance Briefing**

PD-03 dapat menyediakan governance briefing kepada Executive Office  
untuk:

- material governance condition;
- compliance status;
- governance risk;
- unresolved finding;
- policy issue;
- control issue;
- certification concern;
- governance improvement need.

Briefing harus membedakan:

Fact

Evidence

Assessment

Recommendation

Decision

Recommendation tidak boleh dipresentasikan sebagai executive decision.

⸻

**11\. Governance Reporting**

Governance reporting kepada Executive Office harus menyediakan  
informasi yang relevan terhadap executive decision.

Minimum conceptual content:

- governance condition;
- material issue;
- impact;
- evidence;
- applicable requirement;
- current disposition;
- recommendation;
- unresolved dependency;
- required decision, jika ada.

Exact PD-03 executive reporting schema belum ditemukan dalam source.

Karena itu schema tersebut tidak dibekukan sebagai mandatory template.

⸻

**12\. Executive Review Interface**

D2 menyediakan operational interface terhadap executive review.

Model:

Governance Matter

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03 Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Review

&nbsp; ↓

Executive Review — if material/applicable

&nbsp; ↓

Executive Disposition

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03 Operational Follow-through

D2 tidak menggantikan C6 — Architecture/Executive Review Governance  
atau governance review mechanism yang sudah ditetapkan pada Part C.

⸻

**13\. Materiality Boundary**

Tidak seluruh governance activity harus dibawa ke Executive Office.

Executive interface digunakan ketika matter:

- memiliki enterprise-level consequence;
- berada di luar available Platform authority;
- memiliki material cross-platform impact;
- membutuhkan executive decision;
- membutuhkan executive disposition;
- memiliki unresolved governance conflict yang memerlukan authority  
  lebih tinggi.

Exact materiality threshold PD-03 belum ditemukan.

Jangan membekukan numerical threshold tanpa source.

⸻

**14\. Executive Decision Interface**

Jika governance matter membutuhkan executive decision:

PD-03

Analysis

&nbsp; ↓

Recommendation

&nbsp; ↓

Executive Decision Authority

&nbsp; ↓

Decision

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03 Implementation / Governance Follow-through

PD-03 menyediakan governance context.

Executive Office menggunakan applicable executive authority untuk  
decision.

⸻

**15\. Decision vs Recommendation**

D2 harus menjaga separation:

Governance Recommendation

&nbsp; ≠

Executive Decision

PD-03 dapat:

- recommend;
- assess;
- provide options;
- provide impact analysis.

PD-03 tidak boleh menyatakan recommendation sebagai final executive  
decision kecuali authority memang telah didelegasikan secara eksplisit.

⸻

**16\. Governance Escalation Interface**

Jika governance matter tidak dapat diselesaikan pada applicable

Governance Issue

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03 Resolution Attempt

&nbsp; ↓

Unresolved

&nbsp; ↓

C7 Escalation Governance

&nbsp; ↓

Executive Authority, if applicable

&nbsp; ↓

Disposition

D2 menyediakan interface.

C7 tetap menjadi constitutional escalation mechanism.

**17\. Executive Conflict Resolution**

boundary:

Issue

&nbsp;↓

Governance Analysis

&nbsp;↓

Cross-Platform Assessment

&nbsp;↓

Applicable Escalation

&nbsp;↓

Executive Resolution if Required

Executive resolution tidak secara otomatis mengubah domain ownership.

**18\. Executive Governance Evidence**

Evidence dapat mencakup:

- governance standard;
- control evidence;
- compliance assessment;
- governance finding;
- certification evidence;
- cross-platform governance record;
- prior decision;
- impact analysis.

Evidence harus mempertahankan provenance dan historical integrity.

Exact risk taxonomy belum ditetapkan dalam D2 source.

PD-03 dapat menyampaikan kepada Executive Office apabila governance

PD-03

**27\. Ownership Preservation**

Executive Authority

&nbsp; ≠

dan:

Other Platform Execution Ownership

**28\. No Governance Takeover**

Executive interface tidak boleh digunakan untuk:

- mengambil alih domain Platform;
- bypass Architecture Authority;
- bypass Security Authority;
- bypass Quality Authority;
- bypass Runtime Authority;
- bypass applicable governance delegation;
- mengubah organizational ownership secara informal.

**29\. Executive Interface Records**

Material executive governance interaction harus dapat ditelusuri.

Potential record classes:

- Executive Governance Brief;
- Governance Assessment;
- Governance Recommendation;
- Executive Decision;
- Executive Disposition;
- Governance Follow-up;
- Escalation Record.

Nama resmi repository/register belum ditemukan sebagai PD-03-specific  
source.

Karena itu daftar tersebut merupakan artifact classes, bukan  
repository mandate.

⸻

**30\. Interface Control**

Setiap material interface harus memiliki:

- identifiable subject;
- purpose;
- authority context;
- evidence basis;
- responsible owner;
- outcome;
- traceability.

Interface tidak harus berarti meeting atau synchronous communication.

Interface dapat berupa:

- formal report;
- decision package;
- assessment;
- escalation;
- governance record;
- executive review.

Executive Interface

PD-03 Operating Model

kecuali terdapat applicable trigger.

Assessment Context

Evidence

Governance Conclusion

⸻

**38\. Anti-Patterns**

D2 melarang:

AP-01 — Executive Bypass

Melewati applicable governance process hanya karena matter bersifat  
executive.

AP-02 — Executive Takeover

Executive interface digunakan untuk mengambil alih domain execution  
Platform.

AP-03 — Recommendation as Decision

Governance recommendation diperlakukan sebagai executive decision.

AP-04 — Informal Authority Creation

Interaksi executive menciptakan authority yang tidak tercatat pada  
authority model.

AP-05 — Reporting as Ownership

Menerima report dari Platform tidak berarti PD-03 atau Executive Office  
memiliki domain tersebut.

AP-06 — Forced Compliance Conclusion

Executive expectation digunakan untuk memaksa compliance conclusion.

AP-07 — Historical Rewriting

Executive interface digunakan untuk mengubah historical evidence.

AP-08 — Routine Escalation

Seluruh operational matter dinaikkan ke Executive Office tanpa  
materiality basis.

⸻

**39\. Success Criteria**

D2 dianggap berhasil apabila:

1. executive direction dapat diterima oleh PD-03 secara terstruktur;
2. governance interpretation dapat dilakukan tanpa mengubah strategic  
   intent;
3. material governance result dapat disampaikan kepada Executive Office;
4. recommendation dan decision tetap terpisah;
5. executive decision dapat dikembalikan ke PD-03 sebagai authorized  
   disposition;
6. escalation tetap mengikuti C7;
7. governance review tetap mengikuti applicable review authority;
8. PD-03 tidak kehilangan Governance Authority;
9. Platform lain tidak kehilangan domain ownership;
10. executive interface tidak menciptakan hierarchy baru;
11. evidence dan historical records tetap traceable;
12. governance feedback dapat dikembalikan ke operating model;
13. D3 dapat menangani coordination tanpa menduplikasi D2.

⸻

**40\. Canonical Interface Statement**

PD-03 Governance & Compliance interfaces with the Executive Office  
through a controlled governance channel in which executive direction  
and enterprise context are translated into applicable governance work,  
while governance assessment, evidence, material findings, recommendations,  
and required dispositions are returned to the Executive Office through  
the applicable authority, review, and escalation mechanisms. The  
interface does not create subordination, transfer Platform ownership,  
or manufacture authority.

Versi Indonesia:

PD-03 Governance & Compliance berinterface dengan Executive Office  
melalui kanal governance yang terkontrol, di mana executive direction  
dan enterprise context diterjemahkan menjadi governance work yang  
applicable, sementara governance assessment, evidence, material  
findings, recommendations, dan disposition yang diperlukan  
dikembalikan kepada Executive Office melalui authority, review, dan  
escalation mechanism yang berlaku. Interface ini tidak menciptakan  
subordination, memindahkan ownership Platform, ataupun menciptakan  
authority baru.

⸻

**41\. Source-Fidelity Qualification**

SOURCE-SUPPORTED

- PD-03 = Governance & Compliance.
- PD-03 memiliki Governance Authority.
- Policy, Control, Certification, dan Compliance merupakan governance  
  responsibility.
- Executive Office memiliki enterprise-level executive authority.
- Platform mempertahankan domain ownership.
- Governance relationship tidak otomatis menjadi organizational control.
- Dependency tidak sama dengan subordination.

REFERENCE-ADAPTED

- Executive Office → Platform strategic interface.
- Strategic Direction / Enterprise Context sebagai input.
- Executive governance feedback loop.
- Governance reporting interface.
- Executive disposition pattern.

Pola interface Executive Office → Platform secara eksplisit terlihat pada  
reference PD-02.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

SOURCE GAP

Belum ditemukan secara literal:

- PD-03 D2 definition;
- exact executive reporting format;
- exact executive review cadence;
- exact materiality threshold;
- exact executive approval matrix khusus PD-03;
- exact interface artifact/register;
- exact escalation trigger khusus D2.

Semua tetap SOURCE GAP, bukan canonical fact.

⸻

1\. Purpose

D3 menetapkan bagaimana PD-03 mengoperasikan koordinasi Governance &  
Compliance dengan Platform Division lain.

D3 menjawab:

Bagaimana PD-03 berkoordinasi dengan Platform lain ketika governance  
requirement, policy, control, certification, compliance, evidence,  
dependency, atau governance issue melintasi domain Platform?

D3 memastikan koordinasi:

• explicit;

• bounded;

• traceable;

• evidence-based;

• ownership-preserving;

• authority-aware;

• outcome-oriented.

D3 tidak mengubah organizational ownership yang telah ditetapkan pada  
Part A dan Part B.

⸻

2\. Position of D3

D3 berada setelah:

Part C

Governance Architecture

&nbsp; ↓

D1

Governance Operating Model

&nbsp; ↓

D2

Executive Governance Interface

&nbsp; ↓

D3

Platform Governance Coordination

D3 merupakan operating mechanism untuk hubungan PD-03 dengan Platform  
lain.

⸻

3\. Fundamental Coordination Principle

Prinsip utama:

Governance coordination does not transfer Platform ownership.

Model canonical:

PD-03

Governance Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Platform

&nbsp; ↓

Domain Execution

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03 Governance Assessment

Bukan:

PD-03 Governance Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03 Takes Over Platform

Source secara eksplisit mempertahankan domain ownership masing-masing  
Platform dan menyatakan bahwa governance interaction tidak berarti  
organizational control.

⸻

4\. Coordination Constitution

Platform Governance Coordination terdiri atas:

Requirement Coordination

&nbsp; ↓

Dependency Coordination

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence Coordination

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment Coordination

&nbsp; ↓

Finding / Action Coordination

&nbsp; ↓

Resolution Coordination

&nbsp; ↓

Outcome Review

Koordinasi bukan sekadar komunikasi.

Koordinasi harus menghasilkan:

• shared context;

• explicit ownership;

• explicit dependency;

• agreed action;

• traceable outcome.

⸻

5\. Coordination Objectives

PGC-01 — Governance Alignment

Memastikan governance requirement dipahami dan diterapkan oleh Platform  
yang relevan.

⸻

PGC-02 — Ownership Protection

Memastikan setiap Platform tetap menjadi owner domainnya.

⸻

PGC-03 — Dependency Visibility

Memastikan dependency lintas Platform dapat diidentifikasi dan  
ditelusuri.

⸻

PGC-04 — Evidence Availability

Memastikan evidence yang dibutuhkan governance assessment dapat diperoleh  
dari domain owner yang tepat.

⸻

PGC-05 — Compliance Coordination

Memastikan compliance concern lintas Platform dapat dikoordinasikan tanpa  
mengambil alih implementation ownership.

⸻

PGC-06 — Cross-Platform Resolution

Memastikan governance issue yang melibatkan beberapa Platform memiliki  
resolution path yang jelas.

⸻

PGC-07 — Governance Learning

Memastikan hasil koordinasi menjadi input bagi governance improvement.

⸻

6\. Coordination Principles

PGC-P01 — Platform Ownership First

Platform owner tetap memiliki domain ownership.

⸻

PGC-P02 — Governance Without Takeover

PD-03 mengatur governance requirement dan assurance, bukan execution  
domain lain.

⸻

PGC-P03 — Explicit Dependency

Dependency harus dapat diidentifikasi.

⸻

PGC-P04 — Explicit Responsibility

Setiap material action memiliki owner yang jelas.

⸻

PGC-P05 — Evidence Before Conclusion

Governance conclusion harus memiliki evidence yang relevan.

⸻

PGC-P06 — Authority Before Disposition

Disposition harus menggunakan authority yang sesuai.

⸻

PGC-P07 — Coordination Before Escalation

Issue diselesaikan pada level yang tepat sebelum escalation, kecuali  
applicable trigger mengharuskan escalation lebih awal.

⸻

PGC-P08 — Traceability by Design

Koordinasi material harus dapat direkonstruksi.

⸻

7\. Platform Relationship Model

PD-03 dapat memiliki governance interface dengan:

PD-02 — Architecture

PD-04 — Knowledge & Intelligence

PD-05 — Runtime & Execution

PD-06 — AI Engineering

PD-07 — Infrastructure & Platform

PD-08 — Security

PD-09 — Quality & Evaluation

PD-10 — Developer Experience

Source menetapkan Platform tersebut sebagai domain yang mempertahankan  
ownership masing-masing.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

D3 tidak menjadikan daftar tersebut sebagai subordinate structure.

⸻

8\. Coordination Relationship Types

D3 menggunakan beberapa kelas hubungan.

8.1 Governance Requirement Interaction

PD-03 menyampaikan atau mengelola applicability governance requirement.

Governance Requirement

• compliance;

8.3 Dependency Interaction

Platform dapat bergantung satu sama lain dalam memenuhi governance  
requirement.

Provider tetap memiliki capability.

Consumer tetap memiliki ownership atas kebutuhan domainnya.

⸻

8.4 Compliance Interaction

PD-03 dapat melakukan governance compliance coordination dengan Platform  
yang menjadi subject requirement.

⸻

8.5 Certification Interaction

Jika certification membutuhkan domain evidence, PD-03 berkoordinasi  
dengan applicable Platform.

Certification ownership tidak menjadi implementation ownership.

⸻

8.6 Control Interaction

PD-03 dapat menetapkan atau menilai governance control.

Implementation tetap berada pada applicable domain owner.

Source secara eksplisit membedakan Governance Control ownership dari  
Control Implementation.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

9\. Coordination Lifecycle

Material cross-platform governance coordination mengikuti:

Identify

&nbsp; ↓

Scope

&nbsp; ↓

Identify Participants

&nbsp; ↓

Determine Ownership

&nbsp; ↓

Determine Authority

&nbsp; ↓

Define Dependency

&nbsp; ↓

Coordinate

&nbsp; ↓

Collect Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Assess

&nbsp; ↓

Disposition

&nbsp; ↓

Track Action

&nbsp; ↓

Validate

&nbsp; ↓

Close

&nbsp; ↓

Learn

Tidak semua coordination matter memerlukan tingkat formalitas yang sama.

Treatment harus proporsional terhadap materiality.

Exact PD-03 materiality taxonomy belum ditemukan.

⸻

10\. Coordination Intake

Coordination dapat dimulai karena:

• governance requirement;

• policy applicability;

• control requirement;

• certification need;

• compliance assessment;

• governance review;

• cross-platform dependency;

• governance finding;

• unresolved governance issue;

• material governance change.

Input tidak otomatis menjadi governance decision.

⸻

11\. Ownership Determination

Sebelum coordination material dilakukan, harus ditentukan:

0\. siapa domain owner;

0\. siapa governance owner;

0\. siapa action owner;

0\. siapa evidence owner;

0\. siapa decision authority;

0\. siapa reviewer, jika diperlukan.

Model:

Concern

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ├── Governance Responsibility → PD-03

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; └── Domain Responsibility → Applicable Platform

Shared responsibility tidak boleh menghasilkan dual primary ownership.  
Prinsip ini didukung oleh cross-platform source.

Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Divisi yang Akan .txt

⸻

12\. Dependency Declaration

Material dependency harus memiliki:

• provider;

• consumer;

• dependency purpose;

• expected input/output;

• ownership;

• status;

• material impact bila gagal.

Canonical rule:

Dependency

&nbsp; ≠

Ownership

33\. Coordination Materiality

Materiality dapat menentukan:

• participant scope;

34\. Coordination Transparency

Material coordination harus transparan bagi pihak yang memiliki legitimate  
need-to-know.

Transparency mencakup:

• current status;

• ownership;

• dependency;

• decision;

• action;

• outcome.

Transparency tidak berarti seluruh governance information harus terbuka  
kepada semua pihak.

⸻

35\. Coordination Integrity

Coordination dianggap valid apabila:

• scope jelas;

• participants relevan;

• ownership jelas;

• authority jelas;

• evidence tersedia;

• action owner ditentukan;

• outcome dapat ditelusuri.

⸻

36\. Coordination Failure Conditions

Coordination dianggap mengalami failure apabila:

• ownership tidak jelas;

• dependency tidak dikelola;

• evidence tidak tersedia tanpa justification;

• action tidak memiliki owner;

• material finding tidak ditindaklanjuti;

• authority conflict tidak diselesaikan;

• coordination berubah menjadi unauthorized control;

• Platform ownership terancam.

Failure menjadi input bagi applicable escalation atau improvement  
mechanism.

⸻

45\. Anti-Patterns

AP-01 — Coordination as Ownership

Koordinasi tidak berarti ownership.

AP-02 — Governance Takeover

PD-03 tidak mengambil execution domain Platform lain.

AP-03 — Dependency as Subordination

Dependency tidak menciptakan hierarchy.

AP-04 — Evidence Ownership Transfer

Meminta evidence tidak berarti memiliki source domain.

AP-05 — Policy Expansion

Governance Policy tidak sama dengan seluruh technical/domain policy.

AP-06 — Control Takeover

Governance Control tidak sama dengan control implementation.

AP-07 — Certification Takeover

Certification tidak sama dengan implementation.

AP-08 — Informal Decision Authority

Coordination forum tidak boleh menciptakan authority baru.

AP-09 — Executive Bypass

Cross-platform coordination tidak boleh melewati D2/C7 ketika executive  
disposition diperlukan.

AP-10 — Historical Evidence Mutation

Evidence historis tidak boleh diubah demi menghasilkan compliance  
outcome tertentu.

⸻

46\. Coordination Success Criteria

D3 dianggap berhasil apabila:

0\. governance requirement dapat diteruskan ke Platform yang applicable;

0\. ownership Platform tetap jelas;

0\. dependency dapat ditelusuri;

0\. evidence dapat diperoleh secara controlled;

0\. compliance coordination dapat berjalan;

0\. control coordination tidak mengambil implementation ownership;

0\. certification coordination tidak mengambil execution ownership;

0\. cross-platform finding memiliki resolution owner;

0\. unresolved matter dapat dieskalasikan;

0\. executive matter dapat dialihkan ke D2;

0\. governance decision dapat dialihkan ke D4;

0\. coordination record dapat ditelusuri;

0\. tidak ada centralized operational control yang tercipta;

0\. coordination outcome dapat menjadi input improvement.

⸻

47\. Canonical Platform Coordination Statement

PD-03 Governance & Compliance coordinates with AIOS Platform Divisions  
through explicit, evidence-based, traceable governance relationships  
covering policy applicability, controls, certification, compliance,  
dependencies, evidence, findings, and corrective actions, while each  
Platform retains ownership of its domain and execution responsibility.  
Coordination does not create organizational subordination, transfer  
ownership, or manufacture authority.

Versi Bahasa Indonesia:

PD-03 Governance & Compliance melakukan koordinasi dengan Platform  
Division AIOS melalui governance relationship yang eksplisit,  
evidence-based, dan traceable yang mencakup policy applicability,  
control, certification, compliance, dependency, evidence, finding, dan  
corrective action, dengan tetap mempertahankan ownership domain serta  
execution responsibility masing-masing Platform. Coordination tidak  
menciptakan organizational subordination, memindahkan ownership,  
ataupun menciptakan authority baru.

⸻

⸻

**1\. Purpose**

D3 menetapkan bagaimana PD-03 mengoperasikan koordinasi Governance &  
Compliance dengan Platform Division lain.

D3 menjawab:

Bagaimana PD-03 berkoordinasi dengan Platform lain ketika governance  
requirement, policy, control, certification, compliance, evidence,  
dependency, atau governance issue melintasi domain Platform?

D3 memastikan koordinasi:

- explicit;
- bounded;
- traceable;
- evidence-based;
- ownership-preserving;
- authority-aware;
- outcome-oriented.

D3 tidak mengubah organizational ownership yang telah ditetapkan pada  
Part A dan Part B.

⸻

**2\. Position of D3**

D3 berada setelah:

Part C

Governance Architecture

&nbsp; ↓

D1

Governance Operating Model

&nbsp; ↓

D2

Executive Governance Interface

&nbsp; ↓

D3

Platform Governance Coordination

D3 merupakan operating mechanism untuk hubungan PD-03 dengan Platform  
lain.

⸻

**3\. Fundamental Coordination Principle**

Prinsip utama:

Governance coordination does not transfer Platform ownership.

Model canonical:

PD-03

Governance Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Platform

&nbsp; ↓

Domain Execution

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03 Governance Assessment

Bukan:

PD-03 Governance Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03 Takes Over Platform

Source secara eksplisit mempertahankan domain ownership masing-masing  
Platform dan menyatakan bahwa governance interaction tidak berarti  
organizational control.

⸻

**4\. Coordination Constitution**

Platform Governance Coordination terdiri atas:

Requirement Coordination

&nbsp; ↓

Dependency Coordination

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence Coordination

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment Coordination

&nbsp; ↓

Finding / Action Coordination

&nbsp; ↓

Resolution Coordination

&nbsp; ↓

Outcome Review

Koordinasi bukan sekadar komunikasi.

Koordinasi harus menghasilkan:

- shared context;
- explicit ownership;
- explicit dependency;
- agreed action;
- traceable outcome.

⸻

**5\. Coordination Objectives**

**PGC-01 — Governance Alignment**

Memastikan governance requirement dipahami dan diterapkan oleh Platform  
yang relevan.

⸻

**PGC-02 — Ownership Protection**

Memastikan setiap Platform tetap menjadi owner domainnya.

⸻

**PGC-03 — Dependency Visibility**

Memastikan dependency lintas Platform dapat diidentifikasi dan  
ditelusuri.

⸻

**PGC-04 — Evidence Availability**

Memastikan evidence yang dibutuhkan governance assessment dapat diperoleh  
dari domain owner yang tepat.

⸻

**PGC-05 — Compliance Coordination**

Memastikan compliance concern lintas Platform dapat dikoordinasikan tanpa  
mengambil alih implementation ownership.

⸻

**PGC-06 — Cross-Platform Resolution**

Memastikan governance issue yang melibatkan beberapa Platform memiliki  
resolution path yang jelas.

⸻

**PGC-07 — Governance Learning**

Memastikan hasil koordinasi menjadi input bagi governance improvement.

⸻

**6\. Coordination Principles**

**PGC-P01 — Platform Ownership First**

Platform owner tetap memiliki domain ownership.

⸻

**PGC-P02 — Governance Without Takeover**

PD-03 mengatur governance requirement dan assurance, bukan execution  
domain lain.

⸻

**PGC-P03 — Explicit Dependency**

Dependency harus dapat diidentifikasi.

⸻

**PGC-P04 — Explicit Responsibility**

Setiap material action memiliki owner yang jelas.

⸻

**PGC-P05 — Evidence Before Conclusion**

Governance conclusion harus memiliki evidence yang relevan.

⸻

**PGC-P06 — Authority Before Disposition**

Disposition harus menggunakan authority yang sesuai.

⸻

**PGC-P07 — Coordination Before Escalation**

Issue diselesaikan pada level yang tepat sebelum escalation, kecuali  
applicable trigger mengharuskan escalation lebih awal.

⸻

**PGC-P08 — Traceability by Design**

Koordinasi material harus dapat direkonstruksi.

⸻

**7\. Platform Relationship Model**

PD-03 dapat memiliki governance interface dengan:

PD-02 — Architecture

PD-04 — Knowledge & Intelligence

PD-05 — Runtime & Execution

PD-06 — AI Engineering

PD-07 — Infrastructure & Platform

PD-08 — Security

PD-09 — Quality & Evaluation

PD-10 — Developer Experience

Source menetapkan Platform tersebut sebagai domain yang mempertahankan  
ownership masing-masing.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

D3 tidak menjadikan daftar tersebut sebagai subordinate structure.

⸻

**8\. Coordination Relationship Types**

D3 menggunakan beberapa kelas hubungan.

**8.1 Governance Requirement Interaction**

PD-03 menyampaikan atau mengelola applicability governance requirement.

Governance Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Platform

⸻

**8.2 Evidence Interaction**

Platform menyediakan evidence yang diperlukan untuk:

- compliance;
- control assessment;
- certification;
- governance review.

Domain Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03

⸻

**8.3 Dependency Interaction**

Platform dapat bergantung satu sama lain dalam memenuhi governance  
requirement.

Provider tetap memiliki capability.

Consumer tetap memiliki ownership atas kebutuhan domainnya.

⸻

**8.4 Compliance Interaction**

PD-03 dapat melakukan governance compliance coordination dengan Platform  
yang menjadi subject requirement.

⸻

**8.5 Certification Interaction**

Jika certification membutuhkan domain evidence, PD-03 berkoordinasi  
dengan applicable Platform.

Certification ownership tidak menjadi implementation ownership.

⸻

**8.6 Control Interaction**

PD-03 dapat menetapkan atau menilai governance control.

Implementation tetap berada pada applicable domain owner.

Source secara eksplisit membedakan Governance Control ownership dari  
Control Implementation.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

**9\. Coordination Lifecycle**

Material cross-platform governance coordination mengikuti:

Identify

&nbsp; ↓

Scope

&nbsp; ↓

Identify Participants

&nbsp; ↓

Determine Ownership

&nbsp; ↓

Determine Authority

&nbsp; ↓

Define Dependency

&nbsp; ↓

Coordinate

&nbsp; ↓

Collect Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Assess

&nbsp; ↓

Disposition

&nbsp; ↓

Track Action

&nbsp; ↓

Validate

&nbsp; ↓

Close

&nbsp; ↓

Learn

Tidak semua coordination matter memerlukan tingkat formalitas yang sama.

Treatment harus proporsional terhadap materiality.

Exact PD-03 materiality taxonomy belum ditemukan.

⸻

**10\. Coordination Intake**

Coordination dapat dimulai karena:

- governance requirement;
- policy applicability;
- control requirement;
- certification need;
- compliance assessment;
- governance review;
- cross-platform dependency;
- governance finding;
- unresolved governance issue;
- material governance change.

Input tidak otomatis menjadi governance decision.

⸻

**11\. Ownership Determination**

Sebelum coordination material dilakukan, harus ditentukan:

1. siapa domain owner;
2. siapa governance owner;
3. siapa action owner;
4. siapa evidence owner;
5. siapa decision authority;
6. siapa reviewer, jika diperlukan.

Model:

Concern

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ├── Governance Responsibility → PD-03

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; └── Domain Responsibility → Applicable Platform

Shared responsibility tidak boleh menghasilkan dual primary ownership.  
Prinsip ini didukung oleh cross-platform source.

Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Divisi yang Akan .txt

⸻

**12\. Dependency Declaration**

Material dependency harus memiliki:

- provider;
- consumer;
- dependency purpose;
- expected input/output;
- ownership;
- status;
- material impact bila gagal.

Canonical rule:

Dependency

&nbsp; ≠

Ownership

⸻

**13\. Evidence Coordination**

Jika PD-03 membutuhkan evidence:

PD-03 Request

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Integrity / Relevance Check

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Assessment

PD-03 tidak boleh mengubah evidence yang berasal dari Platform lain  
untuk menghasilkan conclusion tertentu.

⸻

**14\. Evidence Ownership**

Evidence ownership tetap mengikuti source domain.

Contoh:

| **Evidence**            | **Primary Owner**        |
| ----------------------- | ------------------------ |
| Architecture evidence   | PD-02                    |
| Security evidence       | PD-08                    |
| Quality evidence        | PD-09                    |
| Runtime evidence        | Applicable Runtime Owner |
| Infrastructure evidence | PD-07                    |
| Governance evidence     | PD-03                    |

PD-03 memiliki governance assessment responsibility terhadap evidence  
tersebut bila applicable, bukan otomatis ownership atas source domain.

⸻

Tidak setiap governance requirement berlaku identik terhadap seluruh

Coordination

Coordination dapat dilakukan melalui mekanisme yang sesuai, misalnya:

D6 — Governance Operating Rhythm

**33\. Coordination Materiality**

Materiality dapat menentukan:

- participant scope;
- evidence depth;
- review depth;
- authority level;
- documentation;
- escalation requirement.

Exact materiality threshold belum ditemukan.

Claude Code tidak boleh menciptakan numerical threshold tanpa source.

⸻

**34\. Coordination Transparency**

Material coordination harus transparan bagi pihak yang memiliki legitimate  
need-to-know.

Transparency mencakup:

- current status;
- ownership;
- dependency;
- decision;
- action;
- outcome.

Transparency tidak berarti seluruh governance information harus terbuka  
kepada semua pihak.

⸻

**35\. Coordination Integrity**

Coordination dianggap valid apabila:

- scope jelas;
- participants relevan;
- ownership jelas;
- authority jelas;
- evidence tersedia;
- action owner ditentukan;
- outcome dapat ditelusuri.

⸻

**36\. Coordination Failure Conditions**

Coordination dianggap mengalami failure apabila:

- ownership tidak jelas;
- dependency tidak dikelola;
- evidence tidak tersedia tanpa justification;
- action tidak memiliki owner;
- material finding tidak ditindaklanjuti;
- authority conflict tidak diselesaikan;
- coordination berubah menjadi unauthorized control;
- Platform ownership terancam.

Failure menjadi input bagi applicable escalation atau improvement  
mechanism.

⸻

**45\. Anti-Patterns**

AP-01 — Coordination as Ownership

Koordinasi tidak berarti ownership.

AP-02 — Governance Takeover

PD-03 tidak mengambil execution domain Platform lain.

AP-03 — Dependency as Subordination

Dependency tidak menciptakan hierarchy.

AP-04 — Evidence Ownership Transfer

Meminta evidence tidak berarti memiliki source domain.

AP-05 — Policy Expansion

Governance Policy tidak sama dengan seluruh technical/domain policy.

AP-06 — Control Takeover

Governance Control tidak sama dengan control implementation.

AP-07 — Certification Takeover

Certification tidak sama dengan implementation.

AP-08 — Informal Decision Authority

Coordination forum tidak boleh menciptakan authority baru.

AP-09 — Executive Bypass

Cross-platform coordination tidak boleh melewati D2/C7 ketika executive  
disposition diperlukan.

AP-10 — Historical Evidence Mutation

Evidence historis tidak boleh diubah demi menghasilkan compliance  
outcome tertentu.

⸻

**46\. Coordination Success Criteria**

D3 dianggap berhasil apabila:

1. governance requirement dapat diteruskan ke Platform yang applicable;
2. ownership Platform tetap jelas;
3. dependency dapat ditelusuri;
4. evidence dapat diperoleh secara controlled;
5. compliance coordination dapat berjalan;
6. control coordination tidak mengambil implementation ownership;
7. certification coordination tidak mengambil execution ownership;
8. cross-platform finding memiliki resolution owner;
9. unresolved matter dapat dieskalasikan;
10. executive matter dapat dialihkan ke D2;
11. governance decision dapat dialihkan ke D4;
12. coordination record dapat ditelusuri;
13. tidak ada centralized operational control yang tercipta;
14. coordination outcome dapat menjadi input improvement.

⸻

**47\. Canonical Platform Coordination Statement**

PD-03 Governance & Compliance coordinates with AIOS Platform Divisions  
through explicit, evidence-based, traceable governance relationships  
covering policy applicability, controls, certification, compliance,  
dependencies, evidence, findings, and corrective actions, while each  
Platform retains ownership of its domain and execution responsibility.  
Coordination does not create organizational subordination, transfer  
ownership, or manufacture authority.

Versi Bahasa Indonesia:

PD-03 Governance & Compliance melakukan koordinasi dengan Platform  
Division AIOS melalui governance relationship yang eksplisit,  
evidence-based, dan traceable yang mencakup policy applicability,  
control, certification, compliance, dependency, evidence, finding, dan  
corrective action, dengan tetap mempertahankan ownership domain serta  
execution responsibility masing-masing Platform. Coordination tidak  
menciptakan organizational subordination, memindahkan ownership,  
ataupun menciptakan authority baru.

⸻

\# 1. Purpose

D4 menetapkan operating mechanism untuk memproses governance matter yang

memerlukan keputusan formal dalam scope PD-03 Governance & Compliance.

D4 menjawab:

\> \*\*Bagaimana governance decision diproses secara authoritative,

\> traceable, accountable, dan bounded?\*\*

D4 menghubungkan:

\`\`\`text

Governance Matter

&nbsp; ↓

Decision Qualification

&nbsp; ↓

Authority Determination

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence & Review

&nbsp; ↓

Decision

&nbsp; ↓

Disposition

&nbsp; ↓

Execution / Follow-up

&nbsp; ↓

Validation

&nbsp; ↓

Closure

D4 tidak menciptakan governance authority baru.

⸻

2\. Operating Architecture Position

Part D mengoperasikan governance architecture yang telah ditetapkan  
pada Part C.

Posisi D4:

Part C — Governance Architecture

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ▼

D1 — Governance Operating Model

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ▼

D2 — Executive Governance Interface

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ▼

D3 — Platform Governance Coordination

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ▼

D4 — Governance Decision Operations

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ▼

D5 — Governance Decision Review

D3 mengatur koordinasi governance matter.

D4 mengatur pemrosesan decision.

D5 melakukan review terhadap decision sesuai scope yang ditetapkan.

⸻

3\. Scope

D4 mencakup governance decisions yang berkaitan dengan responsibility  
PD-03, termasuk:

• Policy;

• Governance Standards;

• Control;

• Certification;

• Compliance;

• Governance Baseline;

• governance exceptions;

• governance findings;

• governance matters yang memerlukan formal disposition.

Baseline PD-03 menetapkan Policy, Control, Certification, dan Governance  
Standards sebagai bagian dari domain/output PD-03.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

4\. Boundary

D4 berada pada boundary:

Governance Matter

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Decision

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Disposition

D4 tidak menjadi:

• technical implementation;

• architecture implementation;

• security execution;

• quality execution;

• operational execution Platform lain;

• domain ownership transfer.

Technical Design secara eksplisit bukan ownership PD-03.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

5\. Fundamental Decision Principle

Governance decision authority must follow documented authority and  
responsibility; consensus alone does not create authority.

Model:

Decision Matter

&nbsp; ↓

Authority Check

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized?

&nbsp; ├── NO → Escalate / Route

&nbsp; └── YES

&nbsp; ↓

Decision Process

⸻

6\. Decision Ownership

Setiap material governance decision harus memiliki Decision Owner.

Decision Owner bertanggung jawab terhadap:

• initiation;

• context preparation;

• impact analysis;

• evidence coordination;

• required consultation;

• decision record;

• disposition tracking;

• follow-up;

• closure.

Decision Owner tidak otomatis merupakan:

• final approval authority;

• reviewer;

• execution owner.

Pemisahan tersebut harus dipertahankan apabila diperlukan oleh authority  
atau segregation-of-duties requirement.

Baseline governance juga menetapkan bahwa setiap keputusan harus memiliki  
Decision Owner yang terdokumentasi.

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

⸻

7\. Decision Qualification

Sebelum diproses, governance matter harus dikualifikasi.

Minimum qualification:

Jika qualification tidak memadai:

Governance Matter

&nbsp; ↓

Insufficient Qualification

&nbsp; ↓

Clarification / Evidence Request

Matter tidak boleh dipaksa menjadi decision hanya untuk memenuhi cadence.

⸻

8\. Decision Authority

Authority harus ditentukan sebelum decision.

Model:

Matter

&nbsp; ↓

Determine Responsibility

&nbsp; ↓

Determine Authority

&nbsp; ↓

Determine Approval Requirement

D4 tidak boleh:

• menciptakan authority baru;

• memperluas authority secara implisit;

• mengubah hierarchy;

• menganggap coordination sebagai authority.

Authority model ditetapkan oleh Governance Architecture, bukan oleh D4.

⸻

9\. Decision Lifecycle

Governance decision mengikuti lifecycle:

Decision Request

&nbsp; ↓

Qualification

&nbsp; ↓

Context & Impact Analysis

&nbsp; ↓

Authority Identification

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence Preparation

&nbsp; ↓

Review / Consultation

&nbsp; ↓

Decision

&nbsp; ↓

Decision Publication / Recording

&nbsp; ↓

Disposition

&nbsp; ↓

Implementation by Applicable Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Monitoring

&nbsp; ↓

Post-Decision Review

&nbsp; ↓

Close or Reassess

Lifecycle ini merupakan bounded operating model.

⸻

10\. Decision Request

Decision request harus menjelaskan:

• apa yang perlu diputuskan;

• mengapa decision diperlukan;

• governance requirement yang relevan;

• affected scope;

• affected Platform;

• decision deadline jika memang ada;

• requested disposition.

Decision request tidak boleh menyembunyikan material uncertainty.

⸻

11\. Context & Impact Analysis

Sebelum decision, context dan impact harus dianalisis sesuai materiality.

Analisis dapat mencakup:

• governance impact;

• compliance impact;

• control impact;

• certification impact;

• architecture impact;

• security impact;

• quality impact;

• cross-platform impact;

• organizational impact.

Tidak semua decision memerlukan seluruh kategori.

Depth analysis harus proporsional terhadap decision impact.

⸻

12\. Evidence Requirement

Decision harus didasarkan pada evidence yang relevan.

Evidence dapat berupa:

• governance requirement;

• policy;

• standard;

• control evidence;

• compliance evidence;

• certification evidence;

• assessment;

• review result;

• authoritative architecture record;

• affected Platform evidence.

Evidence harus dapat ditelusuri terhadap decision.

⸻

13\. Evidence Integrity

Evidence tidak boleh:

• diubah untuk mendukung predetermined outcome;

• dihilangkan karena tidak mendukung recommendation;

• diperlakukan sebagai authoritative tanpa provenance;

• direkonstruksi secara retrospektif tanpa penandaan.

Historical evidence harus dipertahankan.

⸻

14\. Review & Consultation

Decision yang berdampak pada pihak lain harus melibatkan pihak yang  
relevan sesuai authority dan scope.

Consultation dapat diperlukan ketika decision:

• menyentuh Platform lain;

• mengubah governance requirement;

• memengaruhi architecture;

• memengaruhi security;

• memengaruhi quality;

• memengaruhi certification;

• menciptakan cross-platform dependency.

Consultation bukan otomatis approval.

Consultation

&nbsp; ≠

Approval Authority

⸻

15\. Decision Options

Jika terdapat beberapa disposition yang sah, option harus dibuat eksplisit.

Contoh bounded options:

Approve

Approve with Conditions

Defer

Reject

Return for Clarification

Escalate

Daftar ini adalah operational pattern, bukan frozen PD-03 enumeration.

Exact canonical status enumeration harus dikonfirmasi terhadap source  
otoritatif sebelum dimasukkan sebagai repository schema.

⸻

16\. Decision Criteria

Decision harus mempertimbangkan, sesuai relevansi:

0\. applicable governance requirement;

0\. authority;

0\. evidence;

0\. impact;

0\. risk;

0\. cross-platform effect;

0\. compliance implication;

0\. reversibility;

0\. downstream obligation.

D4 tidak menetapkan numerical scoring atau threshold tanpa source.

⸻

17\. Decision Record

Material decision harus menghasilkan record yang memungkinkan traceability.

Minimum conceptual fields:

Decision ID

Decision Subject

Decision Question

Context

Scope

Authority

Decision Owner

Participants

Evidence

Options

Decision

Rationale

Conditions

Actions

Execution Owner

Review Requirement

Status

Traceability

Field tersebut merupakan conceptual minimum, bukan canonical repository  
schema sampai source PD-03 menetapkannya.

⸻

18\. Decision Rationale

Material decision harus memiliki rationale yang cukup untuk menjelaskan:

• requirement yang digunakan;

• evidence yang dipertimbangkan;

• impact yang dinilai;

• authority yang digunakan;

• alasan disposition.

Rationale tidak boleh digunakan untuk menggantikan missing evidence.

⸻

19\. Decision Disposition

Decision harus menghasilkan disposition yang eksplisit.

Conceptual disposition:

Approved

Approved with Conditions

Deferred

Rejected

Returned

Escalated

Closed

Exact enumeration:

SOURCE GAP — NOT FROZEN AS PD-03 CANONICAL ENUMERATION.

Claude Code harus melakukan source reconciliation sebelum enumeration ini  
dijadikan schema formal.

⸻

20\. Conditional Decision

Jika decision disetujui dengan conditions:

setiap condition harus memiliki:

• description;

• owner;

• expected outcome;

• evidence requirement;

• validation requirement;

• status.

Conditional approval tidak boleh diperlakukan sebagai unrestricted approval.

⸻

21\. Decision Communication

Setelah decision sah:

Decision

&nbsp; ↓

Record

&nbsp; ↓

Relevant Parties

&nbsp; ↓

Affected Owners

Communication harus mencapai pihak yang memiliki action atau dependency.

Communication tidak boleh mengubah decision yang telah direkam.

⸻

22\. Decision Execution Boundary

D4 memproses governance decision.

Execution dilakukan oleh owner yang relevan.

PD-03 Governance Decision

&nbsp; ↓

Disposition

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Domain Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Execution

Karena itu:

Governance decision ≠ execution ownership.

PD-03 tidak mengambil alih technical atau operational execution Platform  
lain.

⸻

23\. Cross-Platform Decision

Jika governance decision berdampak pada Platform lain:

Governance Matter

&nbsp; ↓

&nbsp; ↓

Consultation

&nbsp; ↓

Authority Determination

&nbsp; ↓

Decision

&nbsp; ↓

Execution by Applicable Owner

Ownership tetap berada pada Platform yang memiliki domain.

Baseline governance menegaskan bahwa coordination tidak mengubah domain  
ownership.

Governance Decision
