31\. Governance Standards Decision

Governance Standards berada dalam primary output PD-03 berdasarkan baseline.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Decision terhadap Governance Standards harus mempertahankan:

• source traceability;

• applicability;

• authority;

• affected scope;

• version history;

• implementation responsibility.

D4 tidak memperluas Governance Standards menjadi seluruh technical  
standards AIOS.

⸻

32\. Escalation

Decision harus dieskalasikan apabila:

• authority tidak mencukupi;

• terjadi authority conflict;

• terjadi ownership conflict;

• impact melampaui delegated scope;

• decision memerlukan higher authority;

• governance requirement saling bertentangan;

• material cross-platform impact tidak dapat diselesaikan.

Model:

D4

&nbsp;↓

Authority / Scope Insufficient

&nbsp;↓

C7 — Governance Escalation

&nbsp;↓

Applicable Higher Authority

Baseline mendukung escalation berdasarkan impact dan authority boundary.

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

⸻

33\. Delegated Decision

Delegation hanya sah jika authority tersebut memang dapat didelegasikan.

Delegation harus:

• terdokumentasi;

• memiliki scope;

• memiliki boundary;

• memiliki delegate;

• memiliki accountability;

• memiliki duration jika diperlukan;

• dapat ditelusuri.

Prinsip:

Delegation does not create unlimited authority and does not erase  
accountability.

Baseline menegaskan bahwa delegasi tidak mengalihkan tanggung jawab  
governance secara otomatis.

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

⸻

34\. Decision Conflict

Conflict dapat terjadi ketika:

• authority berbeda;

• ownership tidak jelas;

• evidence bertentangan;

• governance requirements conflict;

• decision scopes overlap.

Flow:

Conflict

&nbsp; ↓

Classify Conflict

&nbsp; ↓

Check Existing Authority

&nbsp; ↓

Resolve Within Scope

&nbsp; ↓

Escalate if Required

D4 tidak membuat authority baru hanya untuk menyelesaikan conflict.

⸻

35\. Decision Reconsideration

Decision dapat direconsider apabila terdapat material change seperti:

• new authoritative evidence;

• changed requirement;

• material scope change;

• authority error;

• previously unknown material impact.

Reconsideration harus menghasilkan traceable relationship dengan decision  
sebelumnya.

Historical decision record tidak boleh dihapus untuk menyembunyikan  
decision history.

⸻

36\. Decision Closure

Decision dapat ditutup ketika:

0\. disposition telah direkam;

0\. communication selesai;

0\. required actions selesai atau memiliki accepted disposition;

0\. evidence tersedia;

0\. required validation selesai;

0\. unresolved material issue tidak tersisa.

Closure harus dapat ditelusuri.

⸻

37\. Post-Decision Review

Decision yang material dapat memerlukan review setelah implementation.

Review dapat menilai:

• apakah disposition terlaksana;

• apakah assumptions valid;

• apakah conditions terpenuhi;

• apakah unexpected impact muncul;

• apakah corrective action diperlukan.

Post-decision review menjadi input bagi D5 dan improvement architecture.

⸻

38\. Decision Integrity Controls

D4 harus menjaga:

• authority integrity;

• ownership integrity;

• evidence integrity;

• record integrity;

• traceability;

• independence;

• historical integrity.

Tidak diperbolehkan:

Consensus → invented authority

Coordination → ownership transfer

Decision → automatic execution takeover

Missing evidence → assumed evidence

Historical record → rewritten history

⸻

39\. Decision Anti-Patterns

AP-01 — Authority by Consensus

Consensus tidak menciptakan authority.

AP-02 — Governance Takeover

Governance decision tidak berarti PD-03 mengambil alih execution.

AP-03 — Hidden Approval

Approval informal tidak boleh menggantikan formal authority jika formal  
authority diperlukan.

AP-04 — Decision Without Owner

Material decision tidak boleh tidak memiliki Decision Owner.

AP-05 — Evidence After Decision

Evidence tidak boleh dikumpulkan secara manipulatif setelah outcome  
ditentukan.

AP-06 — Unbounded Delegation

Delegation tidak boleh menciptakan unlimited authority.

AP-07 — Cross-Platform Ownership Capture

Governance coordination tidak boleh mengubah ownership Platform.

AP-08 — Historical Mutation

Historical decision/evidence tidak boleh diubah untuk menghilangkan  
history.

⸻

46\. Operational Decision Quality

D4 dianggap berjalan dengan baik apabila:

• authority dapat ditentukan;

• decision owner jelas;

• evidence memadai;

• affected parties teridentifikasi;

• decision rationale tersedia;

• execution owner jelas;

• record traceable;

• escalation tersedia;

• documented decision ownership;

• authority hierarchy;

• delegation;

• escalation;

• decision lifecycle;

51\. Canonical Freeze Statement

D4 — Governance Decision Operations establishes the controlled operating mechanism through which PD-03 processes governance matters requiring formal decision and disposition, while preserving documented authority, explicit decision ownership, evidence integrity, traceability, cross-platform boundaries, and the separation between governance decision and domain execution.

D4 does not create authority, transfer organizational ownership, replace Architecture Authority, replace Executive Authority, or assume execution responsibility for another Platform Division.

Exact PD-03 decision taxonomy, approval matrix, thresholds, quorum, SLA, forum, voting model, and repository schema remain SOURCE GAP until verified against authoritative PD-03 source.

D4 is therefore FROZEN as a Bounded Canonical Synthesis with Controlled Source Qualification.

\### Freeze verdict

\# 1. Purpose

D4 menetapkan operating mechanism untuk memproses governance matter yang

memerlukan keputusan formal dalam scope PD-03 Governance & Compliance.

D4 menjawab:

\> \*\*Bagaimana governance decision diproses secara authoritative,

\> traceable, accountable, dan bounded?\*\*

D4 menghubungkan:

Decision

Disposition

Closure

D4 tidak menciptakan governance authority baru.

**3\. Scope**

D4 mencakup governance decisions yang berkaitan dengan responsibility  
PD-03, termasuk:

- Policy;
- Governance Standards;
- Control;
- Certification;
- Compliance;
- Governance Baseline;
- governance exceptions;
- governance findings;
- governance matters yang memerlukan formal disposition.

Baseline PD-03 menetapkan Policy, Control, Certification, dan Governance  
Standards sebagai bagian dari domain/output PD-03.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

**4\. Boundary**

D4 berada pada boundary:

Governance Matter

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Decision

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Disposition

D4 tidak menjadi:

- technical implementation;
- architecture implementation;
- security execution;
- quality execution;
- operational execution Platform lain;
- domain ownership transfer.

Technical Design secara eksplisit bukan ownership PD-03.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

**5\. Fundamental Decision Principle**

Governance decision authority must follow documented authority and  
responsibility; consensus alone does not create authority.

Model:

Decision Matter

&nbsp; ↓

Authority Check

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized?

&nbsp; ├── NO → Escalate / Route

&nbsp; └── YES

&nbsp; ↓

Decision Process

**6\. Decision Ownership**

Setiap material governance decision harus memiliki Decision Owner.

Decision Owner bertanggung jawab terhadap:

- initiation;
- context preparation;
- impact analysis;
- evidence coordination;
- required consultation;
- decision record;
- disposition tracking;
- follow-up;
- closure.

Decision Owner tidak otomatis merupakan:

- final approval authority;
- reviewer;
- execution owner.

Pemisahan tersebut harus dipertahankan apabila diperlukan oleh authority  
atau segregation-of-duties requirement.

Baseline governance juga menetapkan bahwa setiap keputusan harus memiliki  
Decision Owner yang terdokumentasi.

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

⸻

**7\. Decision Qualification**

Sebelum diproses, governance matter harus dikualifikasi.

Minimum qualification:

| **Element**       | **Requirement**                   |
| ----------------- | --------------------------------- |
| Subject           | Harus jelas                       |
| Scope             | Harus teridentifikasi             |
| Governance Domain | Harus diketahui                   |
| Authority         | Harus dapat ditentukan            |
| Owner             | Harus ditetapkan                  |
| Evidence          | Harus tersedia atau dapat diminta |
| Impact            | Harus dapat dinilai               |
| Affected Parties  | Harus diidentifikasi              |

Jika qualification tidak memadai:

Governance Matter

&nbsp; ↓

Insufficient Qualification

&nbsp; ↓

Clarification / Evidence Request

Matter tidak boleh dipaksa menjadi decision hanya untuk memenuhi cadence.

⸻

**8\. Decision Authority**

Authority harus ditentukan sebelum decision.

Model:

Matter

&nbsp; ↓

Determine Responsibility

&nbsp; ↓

Determine Authority

&nbsp; ↓

Determine Approval Requirement

D4 tidak boleh:

- menciptakan authority baru;
- memperluas authority secara implisit;
- mengubah hierarchy;
- menganggap coordination sebagai authority.

Authority model ditetapkan oleh Governance Architecture, bukan oleh D4.

⸻

**9\. Decision Lifecycle**

Governance decision mengikuti lifecycle:

Decision Request

&nbsp; ↓

Qualification

&nbsp; ↓

Context & Impact Analysis

&nbsp; ↓

Authority Identification

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence Preparation

&nbsp; ↓

Review / Consultation

&nbsp; ↓

Decision

&nbsp; ↓

&nbsp; ↓

Disposition

&nbsp; ↓

Implementation by Applicable Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Monitoring

&nbsp; ↓

Post-Decision Review

&nbsp; ↓

Close or Reassess

Lifecycle ini merupakan bounded operating model.

⸻

**10\. Decision Request**

Decision request harus menjelaskan:

- apa yang perlu diputuskan;
- mengapa decision diperlukan;
- governance requirement yang relevan;
- affected scope;
- affected Platform;
- decision deadline jika memang ada;
- requested disposition.

Decision request tidak boleh menyembunyikan material uncertainty.

⸻

**11\. Context & Impact Analysis**

Sebelum decision, context dan impact harus dianalisis sesuai materiality.

Analisis dapat mencakup:

- governance impact;
- compliance impact;
- control impact;
- certification impact;
- architecture impact;
- security impact;
- quality impact;
- cross-platform impact;
- organizational impact.

Tidak semua decision memerlukan seluruh kategori.

Depth analysis harus proporsional terhadap decision impact.

⸻

**12\. Evidence Requirement**

Decision harus didasarkan pada evidence yang relevan.

Evidence dapat berupa:

- governance requirement;
- policy;
- standard;
- control evidence;
- compliance evidence;
- certification evidence;
- assessment;
- review result;
- authoritative architecture record;
- affected Platform evidence.

Evidence harus dapat ditelusuri terhadap decision.

⸻

**13\. Evidence Integrity**

Evidence tidak boleh:

- diubah untuk mendukung predetermined outcome;
- dihilangkan karena tidak mendukung recommendation;
- diperlakukan sebagai authoritative tanpa provenance;
- direkonstruksi secara retrospektif tanpa penandaan.

Historical evidence harus dipertahankan.

⸻

**14\. Review & Consultation**

Decision yang berdampak pada pihak lain harus melibatkan pihak yang  
relevan sesuai authority dan scope.

Consultation dapat diperlukan ketika decision:

- menyentuh Platform lain;
- mengubah governance requirement;
- memengaruhi architecture;
- memengaruhi security;
- memengaruhi quality;
- memengaruhi certification;
- menciptakan cross-platform dependency.

Consultation bukan otomatis approval.

Consultation

&nbsp; ≠

Approval Authority

⸻

**15\. Decision Options**

Jika terdapat beberapa disposition yang sah, option harus dibuat eksplisit.

Contoh bounded options:

Approve

Approve with Conditions

Defer

Reject

Return for Clarification

Escalate

Daftar ini adalah operational pattern, bukan frozen PD-03 enumeration.

Exact canonical status enumeration harus dikonfirmasi terhadap source  
otoritatif sebelum dimasukkan sebagai repository schema.

⸻

**16\. Decision Criteria**

Decision harus mempertimbangkan, sesuai relevansi:

1. applicable governance requirement;
2. authority;
3. evidence;
4. impact;
5. risk;
6. cross-platform effect;
7. compliance implication;
8. reversibility;
9. downstream obligation.

D4 tidak menetapkan numerical scoring atau threshold tanpa source.

⸻

**17\. Decision Record**

Material decision harus menghasilkan record yang memungkinkan traceability.

Minimum conceptual fields:

Decision ID

Decision Subject

Decision Question

Context

Scope

Authority

Decision Owner

Participants

Evidence

Options

Decision

Rationale

Conditions

Actions

Execution Owner

Review Requirement

Status

Traceability

Field tersebut merupakan conceptual minimum, bukan canonical repository  
schema sampai source PD-03 menetapkannya.

⸻

**18\. Decision Rationale**

Material decision harus memiliki rationale yang cukup untuk menjelaskan:

- requirement yang digunakan;
- evidence yang dipertimbangkan;
- impact yang dinilai;
- authority yang digunakan;
- alasan disposition.

Rationale tidak boleh digunakan untuk menggantikan missing evidence.

⸻

**19\. Decision Disposition**

Decision harus menghasilkan disposition yang eksplisit.

Conceptual disposition:

Approved

Approved with Conditions

Deferred

Rejected

Returned

Escalated

Closed

Exact enumeration:

SOURCE GAP — NOT FROZEN AS PD-03 CANONICAL ENUMERATION.

Claude Code harus melakukan source reconciliation sebelum enumeration ini  
dijadikan schema formal.

⸻

**20\. Conditional Decision**

Jika decision disetujui dengan conditions:

setiap condition harus memiliki:

- description;
- owner;
- expected outcome;
- evidence requirement;
- validation requirement;
- status.

Conditional approval tidak boleh diperlakukan sebagai unrestricted approval.

⸻

**21\. Decision Communication**

Setelah decision sah:

Decision

&nbsp; ↓

Record

&nbsp; ↓

Relevant Parties

&nbsp; ↓

Affected Owners

Communication harus mencapai pihak yang memiliki action atau dependency.

Communication tidak boleh mengubah decision yang telah direkam.

⸻

**22\. Decision Execution Boundary**

D4 memproses governance decision.

Execution dilakukan oleh owner yang relevan.

PD-03 Governance Decision

&nbsp; ↓

Disposition

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Domain Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Execution

Karena itu:

Governance decision ≠ execution ownership.

PD-03 tidak mengambil alih technical atau operational execution Platform  
lain.

⸻

**23\. Cross-Platform Decision**

Jika governance decision berdampak pada Platform lain:

Governance Matter

&nbsp; ↓

Affected Platform Identification

&nbsp; ↓

Consultation

&nbsp; ↓

Authority Determination

&nbsp; ↓

Decision

&nbsp; ↓

Execution by Applicable Owner

Ownership tetap berada pada Platform yang memiliki domain.

Baseline governance menegaskan bahwa coordination tidak mengubah domain  
ownership.

Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Cabang Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

⸻

**24\. Architecture-Related Decision Boundary**

Jika decision memiliki architecture impact:

Governance Decision

&nbsp; ↓

Architecture Impact

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Architecture Authority

&nbsp; ↓

Architecture Review / Decision

PD-03 tidak menggantikan Architecture Authority.

D4 hanya mengelola governance dimension dari matter tersebut.

⸻

**25\. Security-Related Decision Boundary**

Jika decision memiliki security impact:

Governance Matter

&nbsp; ↓

Security Impact

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Security Authority

&nbsp; ↓

Security Assessment / Input

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Disposition

PD-03 tidak mengambil security ownership.

⸻

**26\. Quality-Related Decision Boundary**

Jika decision membutuhkan Quality & Evaluation:

Governance Matter

&nbsp; ↓

Quality Impact

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Quality Authority

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Decision

Quality ownership tetap berada pada Platform yang relevan.

⸻

**27\. Control Decision**

Untuk control-related governance matter:

Control Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Applicability

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Decision

&nbsp; ↓

Control Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Implementation

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Validation

PD-03 memiliki governance responsibility.

PD-03 tidak otomatis memiliki implementation responsibility.

⸻

**28\. Compliance Decision**

Compliance matter mengikuti:

Compliance Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Finding / Status

&nbsp; ↓

Disposition

&nbsp; ↓

Corrective Action

&nbsp; ↓

Validation

&nbsp; ↓

Closure

Compliance ownership tetap berada pada domain yang relevan apabila  
governance architecture membedakannya.

⸻

**29\. Certification Decision**

Certification matter mengikuti:

Certification Requirement

&nbsp; ↓

Scope

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Certification Disposition

&nbsp; ↓

Conditions / Actions

&nbsp; ↓

Validation

Certification tidak mengubah ownership atas implementation domain.

⸻

**30\. Policy Decision**

Policy decision harus dibatasi pada governance policy scope.

Governance Policy

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03

Technical atau domain-specific policy tetap berada pada applicable domain  
owner.

⸻

**31\. Governance Standards Decision**

Governance Standards berada dalam primary output PD-03 berdasarkan baseline.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

Decision terhadap Governance Standards harus mempertahankan:

- source traceability;
- applicability;
- authority;
- affected scope;
- version history;
- implementation responsibility.

D4 tidak memperluas Governance Standards menjadi seluruh technical  
standards AIOS.

⸻

**32\. Escalation**

Decision harus dieskalasikan apabila:

- authority tidak mencukupi;
- terjadi authority conflict;
- terjadi ownership conflict;
- impact melampaui delegated scope;
- decision memerlukan higher authority;
- governance requirement saling bertentangan;
- material cross-platform impact tidak dapat diselesaikan.

Model:

D4

&nbsp;↓

Authority / Scope Insufficient

&nbsp;↓

C7 — Governance Escalation

&nbsp;↓

Applicable Higher Authority

Baseline mendukung escalation berdasarkan impact dan authority boundary.

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

⸻

**33\. Delegated Decision**

Delegation hanya sah jika authority tersebut memang dapat didelegasikan.

Delegation harus:

- terdokumentasi;
- memiliki scope;
- memiliki boundary;
- memiliki delegate;
- memiliki accountability;
- memiliki duration jika diperlukan;
- dapat ditelusuri.

Prinsip:

Delegation does not create unlimited authority and does not erase  
accountability.

Baseline menegaskan bahwa delegasi tidak mengalihkan tanggung jawab  
governance secara otomatis.

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

⸻

**34\. Decision Conflict**

Conflict dapat terjadi ketika:

- authority berbeda;
- ownership tidak jelas;
- evidence bertentangan;
- governance requirements conflict;
- decision scopes overlap.

Flow:

Conflict

&nbsp; ↓

Classify Conflict

&nbsp; ↓

Check Existing Authority

&nbsp; ↓

Resolve Within Scope

&nbsp; ↓

Escalate if Required

D4 tidak membuat authority baru hanya untuk menyelesaikan conflict.

⸻

**35\. Decision Reconsideration**

Decision dapat direconsider apabila terdapat material change seperti:

- new authoritative evidence;
- changed requirement;
- material scope change;
- authority error;
- previously unknown material impact.

Reconsideration harus menghasilkan traceable relationship dengan decision  
sebelumnya.

Historical decision record tidak boleh dihapus untuk menyembunyikan  
decision history.

⸻

**36\. Decision Closure**

Decision dapat ditutup ketika:

1. disposition telah direkam;
2. communication selesai;
3. required actions selesai atau memiliki accepted disposition;
4. evidence tersedia;
5. required validation selesai;
6. unresolved material issue tidak tersisa.

Closure harus dapat ditelusuri.

⸻

**37\. Post-Decision Review**

Decision yang material dapat memerlukan review setelah implementation.

Review dapat menilai:

- apakah disposition terlaksana;
- apakah assumptions valid;
- apakah conditions terpenuhi;
- apakah unexpected impact muncul;
- apakah corrective action diperlukan.

Post-decision review menjadi input bagi D5 dan improvement architecture.

⸻

**38\. Decision Integrity Controls**

D4 harus menjaga:

- authority integrity;
- ownership integrity;
- evidence integrity;
- record integrity;
- traceability;
- independence;
- historical integrity.

Tidak diperbolehkan:

Consensus → invented authority

Coordination → ownership transfer

Decision → automatic execution takeover

Missing evidence → assumed evidence

Historical record → rewritten history

⸻

**39\. Decision Anti-Patterns**

**AP-01 — Authority by Consensus**

Consensus tidak menciptakan authority.

**AP-02 — Governance Takeover**

Governance decision tidak berarti PD-03 mengambil alih execution.

**AP-03 — Hidden Approval**

Approval informal tidak boleh menggantikan formal authority jika formal  
authority diperlukan.

**AP-04 — Decision Without Owner**

Material decision tidak boleh tidak memiliki Decision Owner.

**AP-05 — Evidence After Decision**

Evidence tidak boleh dikumpulkan secara manipulatif setelah outcome  
ditentukan.

**AP-06 — Unbounded Delegation**

Delegation tidak boleh menciptakan unlimited authority.

**AP-07 — Cross-Platform Ownership Capture**

Governance coordination tidak boleh mengubah ownership Platform.

**AP-08 — Historical Mutation**

Historical decision/evidence tidak boleh diubah untuk menghilangkan  
history.

⸻

dalam authority scope yang tersedia.

D4

Escalation Trigger

&nbsp;↓

D2 menyediakan executive governance interface.

Jika decision membutuhkan executive disposition:

D4

&nbsp;↓

Executive-Level Requirement

&nbsp;↓

D2

&nbsp;↓

Applicable Executive Authority

D4 tidak mengambil alih executive authority.

⸻

**45\. D4 Relationship to Part E**

D4 menghasilkan decision evidence yang dapat menjadi input performance  
review.

Namun D4 tidak menetapkan:

- KPI;
- performance target;
- performance score;
- maturity level.

Concern tersebut berada pada Part E.

⸻

**46\. Operational Decision Quality**

D4 dianggap berjalan dengan baik apabila:

- authority dapat ditentukan;
- decision owner jelas;
- evidence memadai;
- affected parties teridentifikasi;
- decision rationale tersedia;
- disposition eksplisit;
- execution owner jelas;
- record traceable;
- escalation tersedia;
- ownership tetap preserved.

⸻

**47\. Source Qualification**

Baseline yang tersedia mendukung:

- documented decision ownership;
- authority hierarchy;
- delegation;
- escalation;
- decision lifecycle;
- decision traceability;
- governance responsibility;
- separation antara governance dan execution.

Namun belum ditemukan source literal yang secara eksplisit menetapkan  
untuk PD-03 D4:

- exact decision taxonomy;
- exact decision status enumeration;
- exact approval matrix;
- exact quorum;
- exact SLA;
- exact voting model;
- exact decision forum;
- exact repository schema;
- exact decision threshold.

Elemen-elemen tersebut tidak dibekukan sebagai canonical PD-03 facts.

⸻

**48\. Claude Code Completion Directive**

Sebelum melakukan repository hardening atau schema-level implementation,  
Claude Code harus:

1. mencari authoritative PD-03 D4 source;
2. mencari predecessor D3 dan successor D5;
3. memverifikasi decision authority;
4. memverifikasi Decision Owner definition;
5. memverifikasi decision lifecycle;
6. memverifikasi approval model;
7. memverifikasi disposition enumeration;
8. memverifikasi escalation trigger;
9. memverifikasi delegation rule;
10. memverifikasi cross-platform boundary;
11. memverifikasi decision record schema;
12. memverifikasi review mechanism.

Untuk setiap temuan, klasifikasikan:

SOURCE-SUPPORTED

REFERENCE-PATTERN

SOURCE-GAP

Mandatory rule

Jangan mengubah SOURCE-GAP menjadi canonical fact hanya karena pola  
yang sama ditemukan pada PD-01 atau Platform lain.

Jika source tidak ditemukan:

Pertahankan bounded synthesis dan tandai gap untuk refinement berikutnya.

⸻

**51\. Canonical Freeze Statement**

D4 — Governance Decision Operations establishes the controlled operating mechanism through which PD-03 processes governance matters requiring formal decision and disposition, while preserving documented authority, explicit decision ownership, evidence integrity, traceability, cross-platform boundaries, and the separation between governance decision and domain execution.

D4 does not create authority, transfer organizational ownership, replace Architecture Authority, replace Executive Authority, or assume execution responsibility for another Platform Division.

Exact PD-03 decision taxonomy, approval matrix, thresholds, quorum, SLA, forum, voting model, and repository schema remain SOURCE GAP until verified against authoritative PD-03 source.

D4 is therefore FROZEN as a Bounded Canonical Synthesis with Controlled Source Qualification.

\### Freeze verdict

\*\*D4 — Governance Decision Operations: FROZEN.\*\*

D5

Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance

Part D — Governance Operations

D5 — Governance Decision Review

Section ID: D5  
Section Name: Governance Decision Review  
Platform ID: PD-03  
Official Name: Governance & Compliance  
Part: Part D — Governance Operations  
Predecessor: D4 — Governance Decision Operations  
Parent Constitution: Part C — Governance Architecture  
Primary Review Domain: Governance Decision Effectiveness  
Status: FROZEN — BOUNDED CANONICAL SYNTHESIS  
Gold Standard Review: PASS WITH CONTROLLED SOURCE GAP  
Freeze Decision: APPROVED

⸻

1\. Purpose

D5 menetapkan mekanisme operasional untuk meninjau governance decisions  
yang telah diproses melalui D4.

Pertanyaan utama D5:

Apakah governance decision telah diproses, diputuskan, direkam, dan  
ditindaklanjuti sesuai authority, accountability, evidence, governance  
requirement, dan boundary yang berlaku?

D5 tidak membuat decision baru hanya karena sebuah decision sedang  
direview.

Model:

D4 — Governance Decision Operations

&nbsp; ↓

Decision

&nbsp; ↓

D5 — Governance Decision Review

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Finding / Confirmation

&nbsp; ↓

Follow-up / Escalation / Improvement

⸻

2\. Boundary Between D4 and D5

Perbedaan harus eksplisit.

D5 tidak mengambil alih fungsi tersebut.

⸻

3\. Scope Gate

Source-supported scope

Baseline governance secara eksplisit mendukung Governance Review terhadap:

• Governance Constitution compliance;

• Decision Governance effectiveness;

• Delegation compliance;

• Accountability clarity;

• Authority integrity.

Cabang Cabang Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

Dengan demikian D5 secara bounded dapat mencakup review terhadap:

Decision Governance

&nbsp; +

Authority

&nbsp; +

Accountability

&nbsp; +

Delegation

&nbsp; +

Compliance

&nbsp; +

Traceability

Source gap

Belum ditemukan definisi literal PD-03 D5 yang menetapkan:

• exact review cadence;

• exact review classification;

• exact review scoring;

• exact severity scale;

• exact review SLA;

• exact review repository schema;

• exact approval model untuk hasil review.

Elemen tersebut tidak dibekukan sebagai canonical fact.

Scope Gate Result

PASS — Bounded Canonical Synthesis Required

⸻

4\. Review Philosophy

D5 mengikuti prinsip:

Review evaluates governance integrity; it does not become a second  
decision authority merely because it reviews a decision.

Review harus:

• evidence-based;

• traceable;

• proportionate;

• independent apabila diperlukan;

• bounded by applicable authority;

• capable of producing actionable findings.

⸻

5\. Review Independence

Source governance menetapkan Reviewer sebagai pihak yang memberikan  
validasi independen.

Cabang Cabang Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

Karena itu:

Decision Owner

&nbsp; ≠

Reviewer

apabila independence diperlukan.

Reviewer tidak otomatis memiliki authority untuk membatalkan decision.

Reviewer menghasilkan:

• assessment;

• finding;

• recommendation;

• escalation signal.

Disposition terhadap finding mengikuti authority yang berlaku.

⸻

6\. Review Ownership

Baseline governance menetapkan:

Cabang Cabang Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

D5 menggunakan separation tersebut sebagai operating boundary.

⸻

7\. Review Trigger

Governance Decision Review dapat dipicu oleh:

0\. scheduled review;

0\. material governance decision;

0\. material authority action;

0\. material delegation;

0\. governance finding;

0\. compliance concern;

0\. unresolved decision condition;

0\. cross-platform governance impact;

0\. escalation;

0\. material change terhadap applicable requirement.

Exact trigger taxonomy PD-03 belum ditemukan sebagai source literal.

Karena itu daftar di atas merupakan bounded operational trigger model.

⸻

8\. Review Intake

Review intake harus mengidentifikasi:

• Decision ID;

• Decision Owner;

• applicable authority;

• decision subject;

• scope;

• decision date;

• evidence;

• conditions;

• actions;

• affected parties;

• previous review;

• escalation reference jika ada.

Jika informasi minimum belum tersedia:

Review Intake

&nbsp; ↓

Insufficient Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Clarification / Evidence Request

Review tidak boleh menghasilkan conclusion material berdasarkan evidence  
yang tidak memadai.

⸻

9\. Review Scope Determination

Tidak semua decision membutuhkan kedalaman review yang sama.

Review scope ditentukan berdasarkan materiality.

Decision

&nbsp; ↓

Materiality Assessment

&nbsp; ├── Low

&nbsp; ├── Medium

&nbsp; └── High

&nbsp; ↓

Review Depth

Catatan: kategori Low/Medium/High belum merupakan canonical PD-03  
severity enumeration.

⸻

10\. Review Dimensions

D5 minimal menilai dimensi berikut.

10.1 Authority Integrity

Apakah decision dibuat oleh authority yang tepat?

10.2 Accountability Clarity

Apakah Decision Owner dan pihak terkait jelas?

10.3 Delegation Compliance

Apakah delegated authority digunakan sesuai scope?

10.4 Decision Governance Effectiveness

Apakah decision mengikuti governance mechanism yang berlaku?

10.5 Evidence Sufficiency

Apakah evidence cukup untuk mendukung decision?

10.6 Traceability

Apakah decision dapat ditelusuri dari matter sampai outcome?

10.7 Disposition Integrity

Apakah decision menghasilkan disposition yang jelas dan dapat  
ditindaklanjuti?

10.8 Follow-up Integrity

Apakah required actions memiliki owner dan status?

⸻

11\. Authority Integrity Review

Review harus memverifikasi:

Decision

&nbsp; ↓

Authority

&nbsp; ↓

Scope

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Action

Pertanyaan utama:

• siapa yang memutuskan?

• authority apa yang digunakan?

• apakah authority mencakup scope tersebut?

• apakah terdapat delegation?

• apakah delegation valid?

• apakah decision melampaui authority?

D5 tidak menciptakan authority baru untuk memperbaiki authority defect.

⸻

12\. Accountability Review

Baseline accountability membedakan:

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

D5 harus memastikan separation tersebut tidak hilang dalam execution.

⸻

13\. Delegation Review

Delegation review memeriksa:

• delegated authority;

• delegated scope;

• delegate;

• validity;

• duration apabila applicable;

• accountability;

• traceability.

Prinsip:

Delegation does not automatically transfer final accountability.

Jika delegation tidak sesuai:

Delegation Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Corrective Action / Escalation

⸻

14\. Evidence Review

Reviewer harus memastikan bahwa material conclusion didukung evidence.

Evidence review mencakup:

• source;

• provenance;

• completeness;

• relevance;

• consistency;

• traceability.

Reviewer tidak boleh mengubah historical evidence untuk membuat decision  
terlihat compliant.

⸻

15\. Decision Rationale Review

Review harus memeriksa apakah rationale:

• menjawab decision question;

• konsisten dengan evidence;

• menggunakan applicable requirement;

• tidak menyembunyikan material uncertainty;

• tidak mengklaim authority yang tidak tersedia.

Rationale yang lemah menjadi review finding apabila material.

⸻

16\. Compliance Review

Governance Decision Review harus memeriksa compliance terhadap:

• Governance Constitution;

• Decision Governance;

• Authority;

• Accountability;

• Delegation;

• applicable Governance Standards;

• applicable Governance Baseline.

PD-03 memiliki domain responsibility terhadap Policy, Control, Certification,  
dan Governance Standards yang berlaku.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

17\. Decision Traceability Review

Traceability chain:

Governance Matter

&nbsp; ↓

Decision Request

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Analysis

&nbsp; ↓

Authority

&nbsp; ↓

Decision

&nbsp; ↓

Disposition

&nbsp; ↓

Action

&nbsp; ↓

Outcome

&nbsp; ↓

Review

Jika salah satu hubungan material tidak dapat ditelusuri, reviewer harus  
menandainya sebagai evidence gap atau finding sesuai materiality.

⸻

18\. Cross-Platform Decision Review

Jika decision memiliki cross-platform impact:

Decision

&nbsp; ↓

Affected Platform

&nbsp; ↓

Ownership Check

&nbsp; ↓

Authority Check

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Compliance

&nbsp; ↓

Outcome

D5 harus memastikan governance decision tidak berubah menjadi implicit  
ownership transfer.

Prinsip:

Cross-platform governance review does not create cross-platform  
operational ownership.

⸻

19\. Decision Condition Review

Untuk conditional decision:

Decision

&nbsp; ↓

Conditions

&nbsp; ↓

Condition Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Validation

Reviewer memeriksa apakah conditions:

• explicit;

• assigned;

• traceable;

• fulfilled;

• overdue;

• appropriately escalated.

⸻

20\. Decision Outcome Review

Review tidak hanya memeriksa apakah decision dibuat.

Review juga dapat menilai:

• apakah disposition dijalankan;

• apakah outcome sesuai expected condition;

• apakah material unintended effect muncul;

• apakah corrective action diperlukan.

D5 tidak menggantikan Performance Review.

Jika issue berkaitan dengan performance:

Governance Decision Review

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Implication

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Performance Review

⸻

21\. Review Finding

Finding harus digunakan apabila terdapat material deviation dari  
requirement, authority, accountability, evidence, atau process.

Conceptual finding structure:

Finding ID

Decision ID

Requirement

Observation

Evidence

Impact

Owner

Required Action

Due / Review Point

Status

Validation

Exact repository schema masih SOURCE GAP.

⸻

22\. Finding Classification

Finding dapat secara konseptual diklasifikasikan sebagai:

Compliant

Observation

Improvement Opportunity

Non-Compliant

Material Governance Issue

Exact canonical enumeration belum ditemukan.

Claude Code harus mencari source sebelum menjadikannya controlled enum.

⸻

23\. Review Output

Baseline governance menyebutkan output Governance Review:

• Governance Assessment Report;

• Compliance Finding;

• Improvement Recommendation;

• Governance Action Plan.

Cabang Cabang Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

Karena itu D5 menggunakan output structure:

Governance Assessment Report

&nbsp; +

Compliance Finding

&nbsp; +

Improvement Recommendation

&nbsp; +

Governance Action Plan

Tidak semua review harus menghasilkan seluruh artefak tersebut jika  
review tidak menemukan material issue.

⸻

24\. Review Decision vs Governance Decision

D5 harus membedakan:

Review Conclusion

&nbsp; ≠

Original Governance Decision

dan:

Review Recommendation

&nbsp; ≠

Governance Approval

Reviewer tidak boleh mengubah recommendation menjadi decision tanpa  
authority yang sesuai.

⸻

25\. Review Escalation

Escalation diperlukan apabila review menemukan:

• authority conflict;

• material governance violation;

• unresolved accountability;

• invalid delegation;

• material compliance failure;

• unresolved cross-platform conflict;

• issue melampaui reviewer's authority.

Flow:

Review Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Material?

&nbsp; ├── NO → Corrective Action / Improvement

&nbsp; └── YES

&nbsp; ↓

C7 — Governance Escalation Governance

⸻

26\. Executive Interface

Jika finding membutuhkan executive disposition:

D5

&nbsp;↓

Material Governance Finding

&nbsp;↓

Executive Governance Interface

&nbsp;↓

Applicable Executive Authority

D5 tidak menggantikan D2.

⸻

27\. Review Closure

Review dapat ditutup apabila:

0\. scope telah selesai;

0\. evidence telah dievaluasi;

0\. conclusion telah dicatat;

0\. finding telah dicatat jika ada;

0\. required action memiliki owner;

0\. escalation telah dilakukan jika diperlukan;

0\. review record telah disimpan.

Finding yang belum selesai tidak boleh dianggap closed hanya karena review  
period berakhir.

⸻

28\. Corrective Action Verification

Jika review menghasilkan corrective action:

Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Corrective Action

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Verification

&nbsp; ↓

Closure

Reviewer dapat melakukan validation ulang.

Validation ulang tidak boleh dilakukan hanya berdasarkan deklarasi verbal  
jika evidence material diperlukan.

⸻

29\. Review History

Setiap material review harus mempertahankan history.

Minimum conceptual history:

Review Initiated

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence Submitted

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Action

&nbsp; ↓

Validation

&nbsp; ↓

Closure

Historical review record tidak boleh dihapus untuk mengubah governance  
history.

⸻

30\. Review Repository

Review artifacts harus dapat ditelusuri.

Repository minimal harus mampu menghubungkan:

Decision

&nbsp; ↕

Review

&nbsp; ↕

Evidence

&nbsp; ↕

Finding

&nbsp; ↕

Action

&nbsp; ↕

Validation

Exact storage/schema implementation merupakan SOURCE GAP dan harus  
dikonfirmasi oleh Claude Code sebelum repository hardening.

⸻

31\. Review Cadence

Exact PD-03 D5 cadence belum ditemukan dalam source.

Karena itu D5 tidak membekukan:

• weekly;

• monthly;

• quarterly;

• annual;

• event-specific SLA.

Claude Code harus mencari authoritative cadence definition.

Sementara itu:

Review cadence harus ditentukan berdasarkan materiality, governance  
requirement, dan authoritative operating schedule.

⸻

32\. Review Proportionality

Review intensity harus proporsional terhadap:

• decision materiality;

• authority scope;

• cross-platform impact;

• compliance risk;

• reversibility;

• evidence complexity.

Model:

Higher Materiality

&nbsp; ↓

Higher Review Depth

&nbsp; ↓

Higher Evidence Requirement

Ini adalah operating principle, bukan numerical scoring model.

⸻

33\. Review Independence Control

Jika reviewer memiliki conflict of interest:

Conflict Identified

&nbsp; ↓

Reviewer Independence Check

&nbsp; ↓

Reassign / Escalate

Reviewer tidak boleh memvalidasi secara independen suatu decision apabila  
independence requirement secara material dilanggar.

⸻

34\. Review Anti-Patterns

AP-01 — Review as Second Authority

Reviewer tidak otomatis menjadi decision authority.

AP-02 — Rubber-Stamp Review

Review tidak boleh sekadar mengkonfirmasi decision tanpa examination.

AP-03 — Evidence After Conclusion

Evidence tidak boleh dicari secara manipulatif untuk membenarkan  
conclusion.

AP-04 — Finding Without Evidence

Material finding harus memiliki evidence basis.

AP-05 — Finding Without Owner

Finding yang membutuhkan action harus memiliki accountable owner.

AP-06 — Historical Mutation

Historical review record/evidence tidak boleh dimodifikasi untuk  
menghilangkan inconvenient history.

AP-07 — Cross-Platform Takeover

Review tidak memberikan operational ownership kepada PD-03.

AP-08 — Recommendation as Decision

Recommendation tidak boleh diperlakukan sebagai formal decision tanpa  
authority.

⸻

D4:

runs the decision process.

D5:

reviews the integrity and effectiveness of that process.

D4

D5 tidak menjadi duplicate D4.

D5 menghasilkan evidence tentang recurring governance operating issues.

42\. Relationship to Part E

D5 dapat menghasilkan evidence yang menjadi input Performance Architecture.

Namun:

Governance Review

&nbsp; ≠

Performance Review

Governance review menilai governance integrity.

Performance review menilai performance.

Jika satu finding menyentuh keduanya, kedua review tetap mempertahankan  
domain authority masing-masing.

⸻

43\. Governance Decision Review Quality Model

D5 dianggap berkualitas apabila review dapat menjawab:

0\. Apakah authority tepat?

0\. Apakah accountability jelas?

0\. Apakah delegation compliant?

0\. Apakah evidence cukup?

0\. Apakah decision traceable?

0\. Apakah governance requirement dipenuhi?

0\. Apakah disposition dilaksanakan?

0\. Apakah finding memiliki owner?

0\. Apakah unresolved issue dieskalasikan?

0\. Apakah historical record tetap utuh?

Karena literal PD-03 D5 source belum ditemukan, Claude Code wajib

schema atau memperkenalkan enumeration formal.

Required search

1\. Locate authoritative PD-03 D5 source.

12\. Locate corrective action model.

13\. Locate review closure rule.

14\. Locate escalation trigger.

15\. Locate review repository schema.

Classification

Setiap hasil harus diklasifikasikan:

SOURCE-SUPPORTED

REFERENCE-PATTERN

SOURCE-GAP

Mandatory prohibition

Claude Code tidak boleh mengarang:

• review cadence;

• SLA;

• severity threshold;

• numerical scoring;

• quorum;

• reviewer authority;

• cancellation/revocation power;

• repository schema;

• canonical status enumeration.

Jika source tidak ditemukan:

Pertahankan D5 sebagai bounded synthesis dan tandai source gap untuk  
refinement.

47\. Canonical Freeze Statement

D5 — Governance Decision Review establishes the controlled review mechanism through which PD-03 evaluates the integrity, effectiveness, accountability, authority compliance, delegation compliance, evidence sufficiency, and traceability of governance decisions processed through D4.

D5 does not become a second decision authority, does not automatically revoke or replace an existing governance decision, does not transfer operational ownership between Platform Divisions, and does not replace the authority established by Part C.

Where a material finding exceeds the review scope, D5 routes the matter through the applicable escalation or executive governance mechanism rather than creating new authority.

Exact PD-03 review cadence, severity taxonomy, scoring model, SLA, repository schema, and formal review-status enumeration remain SOURCE GAP until verified against authoritative PD-03 source.

D5 is therefore FROZEN as a Bounded Canonical Synthesis with Controlled Source Qualification.

D5 — Governance Decision Review → FROZEN.

**D5**

**Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance**

**Part D — Governance Operations**

**D5 — Governance Decision Review**

Section ID: D5  
Section Name: Governance Decision Review  
Platform ID: PD-03  
Official Name: Governance & Compliance  
Part: Part D — Governance Operations  
Predecessor: D4 — Governance Decision Operations  
Parent Constitution: Part C — Governance Architecture  
Primary Review Domain: Governance Decision Effectiveness  
Status: FROZEN — BOUNDED CANONICAL SYNTHESIS  
Gold Standard Review: PASS WITH CONTROLLED SOURCE GAP  
Freeze Decision: APPROVED

⸻

**1\. Purpose**

D5 menetapkan mekanisme operasional untuk meninjau governance decisions  
yang telah diproses melalui D4.

Pertanyaan utama D5:

Apakah governance decision telah diproses, diputuskan, direkam, dan  
ditindaklanjuti sesuai authority, accountability, evidence, governance  
requirement, dan boundary yang berlaku?

D5 tidak membuat decision baru hanya karena sebuah decision sedang  
direview.

Model:

D4 — Governance Decision Operations

&nbsp; ↓

Decision

&nbsp; ↓

D5 — Governance Decision Review

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Finding / Confirmation

&nbsp; ↓

Follow-up / Escalation / Improvement

⸻

**2\. Boundary Between D4 and D5**

Perbedaan harus eksplisit.

| **Section**                                 | **Primary Function**                                  |
| ------------------------------------------- | ----------------------------------------------------- |
| **D4 — Governance Decision Operations**     | Memproses governance decision                         |
| **D5 — Governance Decision Review**         | Menilai efektivitas dan integrity governance decision |
| **C2 — Architecture Decision Governance**   | Governance framework untuk decision                   |
| **C5 — Architecture Authority Governance**  | Authority boundary                                    |
| **C7 — Architecture Escalation Governance** | Escalation                                            |
| **D9 — Governance Operating Improvement**   | Improvement operating model                           |

D5 tidak mengambil alih fungsi tersebut.

⸻

**3\. Scope Gate**

Source-supported scope

Baseline governance secara eksplisit mendukung Governance Review terhadap:

- Governance Constitution compliance;
- Decision Governance effectiveness;
- Delegation compliance;
- Accountability clarity;
- Authority integrity.

Cabang Cabang Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

Dengan demikian D5 secara bounded dapat mencakup review terhadap:

Decision Governance

&nbsp; +

Authority

&nbsp; +

Accountability

&nbsp; +

Delegation

&nbsp; +

Compliance

&nbsp; +

Traceability

Source gap

Belum ditemukan definisi literal PD-03 D5 yang menetapkan:

- exact review cadence;
- exact review classification;
- exact review scoring;
- exact severity scale;
- exact review SLA;
- exact review repository schema;
- exact approval model untuk hasil review.

Elemen tersebut tidak dibekukan sebagai canonical fact.

Scope Gate Result

PASS — Bounded Canonical Synthesis Required

⸻

**4\. Review Philosophy**

D5 mengikuti prinsip:

Review evaluates governance integrity; it does not become a second  
decision authority merely because it reviews a decision.

Review harus:

- evidence-based;
- traceable;
- proportionate;
- independent apabila diperlukan;
- bounded by applicable authority;
- capable of producing actionable findings.

⸻

**5\. Review Independence**

Source governance menetapkan Reviewer sebagai pihak yang memberikan  
validasi independen.

Cabang Cabang Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

Karena itu:

Decision Owner

&nbsp; ≠

Reviewer

apabila independence diperlukan.

Reviewer tidak otomatis memiliki authority untuk membatalkan decision.

Reviewer menghasilkan:

- assessment;
- finding;
- recommendation;
- escalation signal.

Disposition terhadap finding mengikuti authority yang berlaku.

⸻

**6\. Review Ownership**

Baseline governance menetapkan:

| **Role**                    | **Responsibility**         |
| --------------------------- | -------------------------- |
| Governance Owner            | Memimpin Governance Review |
| Executive Office Leadership | Menetapkan keputusan       |
| Reviewer                    | Independent validation     |
| Custodian                   | Menjaga dokumentasi review |

Cabang Cabang Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

D5 menggunakan separation tersebut sebagai operating boundary.

⸻

**7\. Review Trigger**

Governance Decision Review dapat dipicu oleh:

1. scheduled review;
2. material governance decision;
3. material authority action;
4. material delegation;
5. governance finding;
6. compliance concern;
7. unresolved decision condition;
8. cross-platform governance impact;
9. escalation;
10. material change terhadap applicable requirement.

Exact trigger taxonomy PD-03 belum ditemukan sebagai source literal.

Karena itu daftar di atas merupakan bounded operational trigger model.

⸻

**8\. Review Intake**

Review intake harus mengidentifikasi:

- Decision ID;
- Decision Owner;
- applicable authority;
- decision subject;
- scope;
- decision date;
- evidence;
- conditions;
- actions;
- affected parties;
- previous review;
- escalation reference jika ada.

Jika informasi minimum belum tersedia:

Review Intake

&nbsp; ↓

Insufficient Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Clarification / Evidence Request

Review tidak boleh menghasilkan conclusion material berdasarkan evidence  
yang tidak memadai.

⸻

**9\. Review Scope Determination**

Tidak semua decision membutuhkan kedalaman review yang sama.

Review scope ditentukan berdasarkan materiality.

Decision

&nbsp; ↓

Materiality Assessment

&nbsp; ├── Low

&nbsp; ├── Medium

&nbsp; └── High

&nbsp; ↓

Review Depth

Catatan: kategori Low/Medium/High belum merupakan canonical PD-03  
severity enumeration.

⸻

**10\. Review Dimensions**

D5 minimal menilai dimensi berikut.

**10.1 Authority Integrity**

Apakah decision dibuat oleh authority yang tepat?

**10.2 Accountability Clarity**

Apakah Decision Owner dan pihak terkait jelas?

**10.3 Delegation Compliance**

Apakah delegated authority digunakan sesuai scope?

**10.4 Decision Governance Effectiveness**

Apakah decision mengikuti governance mechanism yang berlaku?

**10.5 Evidence Sufficiency**

Apakah evidence cukup untuk mendukung decision?

**10.6 Traceability**

Apakah decision dapat ditelusuri dari matter sampai outcome?

**10.7 Disposition Integrity**

Apakah decision menghasilkan disposition yang jelas dan dapat  
ditindaklanjuti?

**10.8 Follow-up Integrity**

Apakah required actions memiliki owner dan status?

⸻

**11\. Authority Integrity Review**

Review harus memverifikasi:

Decision

&nbsp; ↓

Authority

&nbsp; ↓

Scope

&nbsp; ↓

Authorized Action

Pertanyaan utama:

- siapa yang memutuskan?
- authority apa yang digunakan?
- apakah authority mencakup scope tersebut?
- apakah terdapat delegation?
- apakah delegation valid?
- apakah decision melampaui authority?

D5 tidak menciptakan authority baru untuk memperbaiki authority defect.

⸻

**12\. Accountability Review**

Baseline accountability membedakan:

| **Layer**   | **Primary Role** |
| ----------- | ---------------- |
| Domain      | Domain Owner     |
| Decision    | Decision Owner   |
| Execution   | Execution Owner  |
| Governance  | Governance Owner |
| Stewardship | Steward          |
| Review      | Reviewer         |
| Custody     | Custodian        |

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

D5 harus memastikan separation tersebut tidak hilang dalam execution.

⸻

**13\. Delegation Review**

Delegation review memeriksa:

- delegated authority;
- delegated scope;
- delegate;
- validity;
- duration apabila applicable;
- accountability;
- traceability.

Prinsip:

Delegation does not automatically transfer final accountability.

Jika delegation tidak sesuai:

Delegation Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Corrective Action / Escalation

⸻

**14\. Evidence Review**

Reviewer harus memastikan bahwa material conclusion didukung evidence.

Evidence review mencakup:

- source;
- provenance;
- completeness;
- relevance;
- consistency;
- traceability.

Reviewer tidak boleh mengubah historical evidence untuk membuat decision  
terlihat compliant.

⸻

**15\. Decision Rationale Review**

Review harus memeriksa apakah rationale:

- menjawab decision question;
- konsisten dengan evidence;
- menggunakan applicable requirement;
- tidak menyembunyikan material uncertainty;
- tidak mengklaim authority yang tidak tersedia.

Rationale yang lemah menjadi review finding apabila material.

⸻

**16\. Compliance Review**

Governance Decision Review harus memeriksa compliance terhadap:

- Governance Constitution;
- Decision Governance;
- Authority;
- Accountability;
- Delegation;
- applicable Governance Standards;
- applicable Governance Baseline.

PD-03 memiliki domain responsibility terhadap Policy, Control, Certification,  
dan Governance Standards yang berlaku.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

⸻

Traceability chain:

Authority

&nbsp; ↓

Decision

&nbsp; ↓

Disposition

&nbsp; ↓

Action

&nbsp; ↓

Outcome

&nbsp; ↓

Review

Jika salah satu hubungan material tidak dapat ditelusuri, reviewer harus  
menandainya sebagai evidence gap atau finding sesuai materiality.

**18\. Cross-Platform Decision Review**

Jika decision memiliki cross-platform impact:

Decision

&nbsp; ↓

Affected Platform

&nbsp; ↓

Ownership Check

Cross-platform governance review does not create cross-platform

**19\. Decision Condition Review**

Untuk conditional decision:

Decision

Conditions

Validation

Reviewer memeriksa apakah conditions:

- explicit;

- traceable;

- overdue;
- appropriately escalated.

⸻

**20\. Decision Outcome Review**

Review tidak hanya memeriksa apakah decision dibuat.

Review juga dapat menilai:

- apakah disposition dijalankan;
- apakah outcome sesuai expected condition;
- apakah material unintended effect muncul;
- apakah corrective action diperlukan.

D5 tidak menggantikan Performance Review.

Jika issue berkaitan dengan performance:

Governance Decision Review

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Implication

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Performance Review

**21\. Review Finding**

Finding harus digunakan apabila terdapat material deviation dari

Finding ID

Decision ID

Requirement

Observation

Evidence

Impact

Owner

Due / Review Point

Baseline governance menyebutkan output Governance Review:

- Improvement Recommendation;
- Governance Action Plan.

Cabang Cabang Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

Karena itu D5 menggunakan output structure:

Governance Assessment Report

&nbsp; +

Compliance Finding

&nbsp; +

Improvement Recommendation

&nbsp; +

Governance Action Plan

Tidak semua review harus menghasilkan seluruh artefak tersebut jika  
review tidak menemukan material issue.

⸻

**24\. Review Decision vs Governance Decision**

D5 harus membedakan:

Review Conclusion

&nbsp; ≠

Original Governance Decision

dan:

Review Recommendation

&nbsp; ≠

Governance Approval

Reviewer tidak boleh mengubah recommendation menjadi decision tanpa  
authority yang sesuai.

⸻

**25\. Review Escalation**

Escalation diperlukan apabila review menemukan:

- authority conflict;
- material governance violation;
- unresolved accountability;
- invalid delegation;
- material compliance failure;
- unresolved cross-platform conflict;
- issue melampaui reviewer's authority.

Flow:

Review Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Material?

&nbsp; ├── NO → Corrective Action / Improvement

&nbsp; └── YES

&nbsp; ↓

C7 — Governance Escalation Governance

⸻

**26\. Executive Interface**

Jika finding membutuhkan executive disposition:

D5

&nbsp;↓

Material Governance Finding

&nbsp;↓

Executive Governance Interface

&nbsp;↓

Applicable Executive Authority

D5 tidak menggantikan D2.

⸻

**27\. Review Closure**

Review dapat ditutup apabila:

1. scope telah selesai;
2. evidence telah dievaluasi;
3. conclusion telah dicatat;
4. finding telah dicatat jika ada;
5. required action memiliki owner;
6. escalation telah dilakukan jika diperlukan;
7. review record telah disimpan.

Finding yang belum selesai tidak boleh dianggap closed hanya karena review  
period berakhir.

⸻

**28\. Corrective Action Verification**

Jika review menghasilkan corrective action:

Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Corrective Action

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Verification

&nbsp; ↓

Closure

Reviewer dapat melakukan validation ulang.

Validation ulang tidak boleh dilakukan hanya berdasarkan deklarasi verbal  
jika evidence material diperlukan.

⸻

**29\. Review History**

Setiap material review harus mempertahankan history.

Minimum conceptual history:

Review Initiated

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence Submitted

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Action

&nbsp; ↓

Validation

&nbsp; ↓

Closure

Historical review record tidak boleh dihapus untuk mengubah governance  
history.

⸻

**30\. Review Repository**

Review artifacts harus dapat ditelusuri.

Repository minimal harus mampu menghubungkan:

Decision

&nbsp; ↕

Review

&nbsp; ↕

Evidence

&nbsp; ↕

Finding

&nbsp; ↕

Action

&nbsp; ↕

Validation

Exact storage/schema implementation merupakan SOURCE GAP dan harus  
dikonfirmasi oleh Claude Code sebelum repository hardening.

⸻

**31\. Review Cadence**

Exact PD-03 D5 cadence belum ditemukan dalam source.

Karena itu D5 tidak membekukan:

- weekly;
- monthly;
- quarterly;
- annual;
- event-specific SLA.

Claude Code harus mencari authoritative cadence definition.

Sementara itu:

Review cadence harus ditentukan berdasarkan materiality, governance  
requirement, dan authoritative operating schedule.

⸻

**32\. Review Proportionality**

Review intensity harus proporsional terhadap:

- decision materiality;
- authority scope;
- cross-platform impact;
- compliance risk;
- reversibility;
- evidence complexity.

Model:

Higher Materiality

&nbsp; ↓

Higher Review Depth

&nbsp; ↓

Higher Evidence Requirement

Ini adalah operating principle, bukan numerical scoring model.

⸻

**33\. Review Independence Control**

Jika reviewer memiliki conflict of interest:

Conflict Identified

&nbsp; ↓

Reviewer Independence Check

&nbsp; ↓

Reassign / Escalate

Reviewer tidak boleh memvalidasi secara independen suatu decision apabila  
independence requirement secara material dilanggar.

⸻

**34\. Review Anti-Patterns**

**AP-01 — Review as Second Authority**

Reviewer tidak otomatis menjadi decision authority.

**AP-02 — Rubber-Stamp Review**

Review tidak boleh sekadar mengkonfirmasi decision tanpa examination.

**AP-03 — Evidence After Conclusion**

Evidence tidak boleh dicari secara manipulatif untuk membenarkan  
conclusion.

**AP-04 — Finding Without Evidence**

Material finding harus memiliki evidence basis.

**AP-05 — Finding Without Owner**

Finding yang membutuhkan action harus memiliki accountable owner.

**AP-06 — Historical Mutation**

Historical review record/evidence tidak boleh dimodifikasi untuk  
menghilangkan inconvenient history.

**AP-07 — Cross-Platform Takeover**

Review tidak memberikan operational ownership kepada PD-03.

**AP-08 — Recommendation as Decision**

Recommendation tidak boleh diperlakukan sebagai formal decision tanpa  
authority.

⸻

**35\. Relationship to D4**

D4:

runs the decision process.

D5:

reviews the integrity and effectiveness of that process.

D4

Decision Operations

&nbsp; ↓

Decision

&nbsp; ↓

D5

Decision Review

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Finding / Confirmation

D5 tidak menjadi duplicate D4.

⸻

C2 menetapkan governance architecture untuk decision.

D5 memeriksa apakah decision operation mematuhi architecture tersebut.

C2

Decision Governance

D4

&nbsp; ↓

D5

C4 menetapkan accountability governance.

D5 memeriksa apakah accountability tetap jelas dalam actual decision  
operation.

Accountability

&nbsp; ↓

D4

Decision

&nbsp; ↓

C5 menetapkan authority governance.

C5

Authority

**40\. Relationship to C7**

C7 menyediakan escalation governance.

D5

&nbsp; ↓

&nbsp; ↓

⸻

**41\. Relationship to D9**

D5 menghasilkan evidence tentang recurring governance operating issues.

Jika issue berulang:

D5 Review Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Pattern Detected

&nbsp; ↓

D9 — Governance Operating Improvement

D5 menemukan improvement signal.

D9 mengelola improvement terhadap operating model.

⸻

**42\. Relationship to Part E**

D5 dapat menghasilkan evidence yang menjadi input Performance Architecture.

Namun:

Governance Review

&nbsp; ≠

Performance Review

Governance review menilai governance integrity.

Performance review menilai performance.

Jika satu finding menyentuh keduanya, kedua review tetap mempertahankan  
domain authority masing-masing.

⸻

**43\. Governance Decision Review Quality Model**

D5 dianggap berkualitas apabila review dapat menjawab:

1. Apakah authority tepat?
2. Apakah accountability jelas?
3. Apakah delegation compliant?
4. Apakah evidence cukup?
5. Apakah decision traceable?
6. Apakah governance requirement dipenuhi?
7. Apakah disposition dilaksanakan?
8. Apakah finding memiliki owner?
9. Apakah unresolved issue dieskalasikan?
10. Apakah historical record tetap utuh?

⸻

**44\. Claude Code Completion Directive**

Karena literal PD-03 D5 source belum ditemukan, Claude Code wajib  
melakukan source reconciliation sebelum mengubah D5 menjadi repository  
schema atau memperkenalkan enumeration formal.

Required search

1\. Locate authoritative PD-03 D5 source.

2\. Locate explicit D4 → D5 dependency.

3\. Locate Governance Decision Review definition.

4\. Locate Governance Review Owner.

5\. Locate Reviewer / independence rule.

6\. Locate review trigger.

7\. Locate review cadence.

8\. Locate review criteria.

9\. Locate finding taxonomy.

10\. Locate severity model.

11\. Locate review output schema.

12\. Locate corrective action model.

13\. Locate review closure rule.

14\. Locate escalation trigger.

15\. Locate review repository schema.

Classification

Setiap hasil harus diklasifikasikan:

SOURCE-SUPPORTED

REFERENCE-PATTERN

SOURCE-GAP

Mandatory prohibition

Claude Code tidak boleh mengarang:

- review cadence;
- SLA;
- severity threshold;
- numerical scoring;
- quorum;
- reviewer authority;
- cancellation/revocation power;
- repository schema;
- canonical status enumeration.

Jika source tidak ditemukan:

Pertahankan D5 sebagai bounded synthesis dan tandai source gap untuk  
refinement.

**47\. Canonical Freeze Statement**

D5 — Governance Decision Review establishes the controlled review mechanism through which PD-03 evaluates the integrity, effectiveness, accountability, authority compliance, delegation compliance, evidence sufficiency, and traceability of governance decisions processed through D4.

D5 does not become a second decision authority, does not automatically revoke or replace an existing governance decision, does not transfer operational ownership between Platform Divisions, and does not replace the authority established by Part C.

Where a material finding exceeds the review scope, D5 routes the matter through the applicable escalation or executive governance mechanism rather than creating new authority.

Exact PD-03 review cadence, severity taxonomy, scoring model, SLA, repository schema, and formal review-status enumeration remain SOURCE GAP until verified against authoritative PD-03 source.

D5 is therefore FROZEN as a Bounded Canonical Synthesis with Controlled Source Qualification.

D5 — Governance Decision Review → FROZEN.

D6

Successor: D7 — Governance Collaboration

1\. Purpose

D6 menetapkan temporal operating architecture bagi Governance & Compliance.

D6 menjawab pertanyaan:

"Dengan ritme apa governance review, governance coordination, decision review, compliance activity, dan governance follow-up dijalankan secara konsisten, traceable, dan proporsional?"

D6 bukan sekadar kalender meeting.

D6 merupakan mekanisme untuk menyelaraskan:

Governance Work

&nbsp; ↓

Review

&nbsp; ↓

Decision

&nbsp; ↓

Compliance

&nbsp; ↓

Follow-up

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement

dalam dimensi waktu.

⸻

2\. Position in Part D

D6 berada setelah D5 karena rhythm harus mengoperasikan review mechanism yang telah ditetapkan sebelumnya.

D1 — Governance Operating Model

&nbsp; ↓

D2 — Executive Governance Interface

&nbsp; ↓

D3 — Platform Governance Coordination

&nbsp; ↓

D4 — Governance Decision Operations

&nbsp; ↓

D5 — Governance Decision Review

&nbsp; ↓

D6 — Governance Review & Operating Rhythm

&nbsp; ↓

D7 — Governance Collaboration

&nbsp; ↓

D8 — Governance Operational Control

&nbsp; ↓

D9 — Governance Operating Improvement

&nbsp; ↓

D10 — Governance Operating Success

D6 tidak menggantikan D5.

D5 menentukan bagaimana review dilakukan.

D6 menentukan bagaimana review dan governance operations disinkronkan  
dalam waktu.

⸻

3\. Scope Gate

D6 mencakup temporal coordination untuk:

• governance review;

• decision review;

• compliance activity;

• governance assessment;

• governance coordination;

• governance follow-up;

• governance reporting;

• governance improvement feedback.

D6 tidak menetapkan:

• governance authority;

• decision authority;

• organizational structure;

• policy substance;

• control implementation;

• certification implementation;

• KPI;

• performance targets;

• technical execution.

⸻

4\. Fundamental Rhythm Principle

Governance rhythm exists to synchronize governance work; it does not  
create new authority or ownership.

Dengan demikian:

Rhythm

&nbsp; ≠

Authority

Rhythm

&nbsp; ≠

Ownership

Rhythm

&nbsp; ≠

Decision Right

Jadwal atau cadence tidak dapat menciptakan authority yang tidak diberikan  
oleh Part C.

⸻

5\. Governance Rhythm Architecture

D6 menggunakan layered temporal model:

Strategic / Governance Direction

&nbsp; ↓

Annual Governance Cycle

&nbsp; ↓

Quarterly Governance Cycle

&nbsp; ↓

Monthly Governance Cycle

&nbsp; ↓

Weekly Governance Cycle

&nbsp; ↓

Daily / Event-Driven Governance Operations

Struktur multi-cycle ini berasal dari reference operating-rhythm model yang  
menempatkan daily, weekly, monthly, quarterly, dan annual cycle sebagai  
lapisan temporal architecture.

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

Namun, cadence spesifik PD-03 tidak dibekukan dari reference tersebut.

⸻

6\. Annual Governance Rhythm

Annual cycle digunakan untuk aktivitas governance yang membutuhkan  
perspektif tahunan.

Secara konseptual dapat mencakup:

• governance operating review;

• governance framework review;

• annual governance priorities;

• governance maturity assessment;

• annual improvement direction;

• annual governance planning.

Exact annual events PD-03:

SOURCE GAP — NOT FROZEN.

Claude Code harus mencari authoritative PD-03 schedule sebelum menetapkan  
event atau tanggal tertentu.

⸻

7\. Quarterly Governance Rhythm

Quarterly cycle digunakan untuk governance matters yang membutuhkan  
periodic strategic assessment.

Potential scope:

Quarterly Governance Review

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Findings

&nbsp; ↓

Risk / Compliance Position

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement Priorities

Quarterly rhythm tidak otomatis berarti PD-03 wajib mengadakan meeting  
quarterly.

Frekuensi formal harus berasal dari applicable authority atau operating  
schedule.

⸻

8\. Monthly Governance Rhythm

Monthly cycle dapat digunakan untuk:

• governance review;

• unresolved finding review;

• compliance status;

• decision follow-up;

• governance action tracking;

• cross-platform governance coordination.

Namun:

Exact monthly cadence is not frozen as a PD-03 canonical requirement.

⸻

9\. Weekly Governance Rhythm

Weekly cycle dapat digunakan untuk operational synchronization ketika  
governance work memiliki active matters.

Contoh concern:

• open governance decisions;

• active reviews;

• compliance actions;

• escalations;

• dependencies;

• evidence requests.

Weekly rhythm bersifat operational pattern, bukan mandatory PD-03 meeting  
schedule.

⸻

10\. Daily / Event-Driven Rhythm

Tidak semua governance work mengikuti kalender.

Material governance events dapat memicu activity di luar regular cycle.

Trigger dapat berupa:

• material governance issue;

• urgent compliance matter;

• authority conflict;

• escalation;

• material decision;

• critical finding;

• external requirement;

• significant change.

Model:

Regular Rhythm

&nbsp; +

Event Trigger

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Action

Event-driven operation tidak boleh digunakan untuk bypass governance  
requirements.

⸻

11\. Rhythm Selection Principle

Tidak semua governance activity harus mengikuti semua temporal layers.

Selection harus mempertimbangkan:

• materiality;

• urgency;

• governance impact;

• compliance impact;

• cross-platform impact;

• authority requirement;

• review requirement;

• dependency;

• existing governance schedule.

Model:

Materiality

&nbsp; ↓

Required Rhythm

&nbsp; ↓

Review Depth

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence Requirement

⸻

12\. Rhythm and Materiality

Higher-impact governance matters dapat membutuhkan tighter rhythm.

Low Materiality

&nbsp; ↓

Normal Cycle

Material Governance Matter

&nbsp; ↓

Accelerated / Event-Driven Cycle

Critical / Escalated Matter

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Escalation Rhythm

Tidak boleh dibuat numerical threshold tanpa source.

Review

Finding

Action

Decision

&nbsp; ↓

Governance assessment harus memiliki planned review point.

Assessment dapat menghasilkan:

• governance assessment;

Source baseline memang mengidentifikasi Governance Review sebagai aktivitas  
periodik dan menghasilkan assessment/finding/improvement outputs.

Finding

&nbsp; ↓

Owner

Action

Progress Review

Validation

18\. Governance Action Rhythm

Governance actions harus masuk ke operational tracking.

Setiap material action minimal memiliki:

• action identity;

SOURCE GAP — NOT FROZEN.

19\. Escalation Rhythm

Escalation tidak selalu menunggu regular cycle.

Jika materiality atau authority condition mengharuskan escalation:

Trigger

&nbsp; ↓

Immediate Qualification

&nbsp; ↓

Escalation

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Authority

20\. Executive Governance Rhythm

Executive governance interface harus mengikuti authority yang ditetapkan

D6 hanya mengatur synchronization point.

Governance Matter

Executive Governance Interface

Applicable Executive Authority

21\. Cross-Platform Governance Rhythm

Cross-platform governance membutuhkan synchronization mechanism.

Model:

PD-03 Governance Matter

Affected Platform

Coordination Point

Evidence / Review

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Decision

&nbsp; ↓

22\. Governance Reporting Rhythm

Governance reporting harus mengikuti relevant review cycle.

Potential outputs:

• governance status;

• compliance status;

• open findings;

• decision status;

• corrective action status;

• escalation status;

• improvement status.

Exact report cadence:

SOURCE GAP.

⸻

23\. Rhythm Synchronization

Berbagai governance cycles harus disinkronkan agar tidak menciptakan  
duplicate work.

Compliance Cycle

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ├──── Governance Review

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ├──── Decision Review

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ├──── Executive Interface

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; └──── Improvement Cycle

Jika dua review memiliki overlap material, operating owner harus  
mengonsolidasikan evidence apabila governance requirements mengizinkan.

⸻

24\. Avoiding Review Duplication

D6 harus mencegah:

• evidence dikumpulkan berulang tanpa alasan;

• review yang sama dijalankan dua kali;

• conflicting cadence;

• duplicate reporting;

• duplicate finding registers.

Namun consolidation tidak boleh menghilangkan requirement yang diwajibkan  
oleh authority berbeda.

⸻

25\. Rhythm Ownership

Operating rhythm memiliki owner yang bertanggung jawab memastikan cycle  
berjalan.

Namun:

Rhythm Owner

&nbsp; ≠

Decision Authority

Rhythm Owner bertanggung jawab atas coordination of timing, bukan terhadap  
seluruh governance decision.

Exact PD-03 role title:

SOURCE GAP — NOT FROZEN.

⸻

26\. Calendar Integrity

Governance calendar harus menjaga:

• review dependency;

• decision dependency;

• evidence preparation;

• action deadlines;

• escalation timing;

• validation timing.

Calendar tidak boleh menjadi sumber authority baru.

⸻

27\. Schedule Change

Perubahan rhythm harus mempertimbangkan:

• affected governance activity;

• dependency;

• authority;

• downstream review;

• compliance requirement;

• affected Platforms.

Material rhythm change harus traceable.

Operating convenience saja tidak cukup untuk membatalkan mandatory  
governance requirement.

⸻

28\. Emergency Governance Rhythm

Emergency governance matter dapat menggunakan accelerated operating rhythm.

Emergency Trigger

&nbsp; ↓

Qualification

&nbsp; ↓

Applicable Authority

&nbsp; ↓

Accelerated Review

&nbsp; ↓

Decision / Escalation

&nbsp; ↓

Post-Event Review

Emergency rhythm:

• tidak menciptakan authority baru;

• tidak menghapus mandatory accountability;

• tidak menghapus traceability;

• tidak mengubah historical evidence.

Exact emergency SLA:

SOURCE GAP.

⸻

29\. Deferred Matter

Jika matter tidak dapat diselesaikan dalam cycle saat ini:

Matter

&nbsp;↓

Deferred

&nbsp;↓

Reason

&nbsp;↓

Owner

&nbsp;↓

Next Review Point

Deferment harus memiliki alasan yang dapat ditelusuri.

"Deferred" tidak boleh digunakan untuk menyembunyikan unresolved governance  
issue.

40\. Anti-Patterns

AP-01 — Calendar as Authority

Jadwal tidak menciptakan decision authority.

AP-02 — Mandatory Meeting Inflation

Tidak semua governance matter harus memiliki meeting.

AP-03 — Cadence as Compliance

Mengikuti jadwal tidak otomatis berarti compliant.

AP-04 — Review Deferral Abuse

Regular cycle tidak boleh digunakan untuk menyembunyikan material issue.

AP-05 — Cross-Platform Ownership Drift

Coordination rhythm tidak memindahkan ownership.

AP-06 — Duplicate Review

Review yang sama tidak boleh dijalankan berulang tanpa material purpose.

AP-07 — Historical Mutation

Rhythm adjustment tidak boleh mengubah historical evidence.

AP-08 — Informal Emergency Authority

Emergency rhythm tidak boleh menciptakan informal authority.

Source-supported

53\. Canonical Freeze Statement

D6 — Governance Review & Operating Rhythm establishes the temporal operating architecture through which PD-03 synchronizes governance review, decision review, compliance activity, governance assessment, follow-up, reporting, and improvement while preserving the authority, accountability, ownership, and governance boundaries established by Parts A–C and D1–D5.

D6 does not create authority, transfer ownership, replace Governance Decision Review, replace Executive Authority, or impose operational control over another Platform Division.

Daily, weekly, monthly, quarterly, annual, and event-driven layers are retained as temporal architecture patterns; exact PD-03 cadence, SLA, forum, calendar, rhythm owner, attendance, and repository schema remain SOURCE GAP until verified against authoritative PD-03 source.

D6 is therefore FROZEN as a Bounded Canonical Synthesis with Controlled Source Qualification.

Final status: D6 — FROZEN.

**1\. Purpose**

D6 menetapkan temporal operating architecture bagi Governance & Compliance.

D6 menjawab pertanyaan:

"Dengan ritme apa governance review, governance coordination, decision review, compliance activity, dan governance follow-up dijalankan secara konsisten, traceable, dan proporsional?"

D6 bukan sekadar kalender meeting.

D6 merupakan mekanisme untuk menyelaraskan:

Governance Work

&nbsp; ↓

Review

&nbsp; ↓

Decision

&nbsp; ↓

Compliance

&nbsp; ↓

Follow-up

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement

dalam dimensi waktu.

⸻

**2\. Position in Part D**

D6 berada setelah D5 karena rhythm harus mengoperasikan review mechanism yang telah ditetapkan sebelumnya.

D1 — Governance Operating Model

&nbsp; ↓

D2 — Executive Governance Interface

&nbsp; ↓

D3 — Platform Governance Coordination

&nbsp; ↓

D4 — Governance Decision Operations

&nbsp; ↓

D5 — Governance Decision Review

&nbsp; ↓

D6 — Governance Review & Operating Rhythm

&nbsp; ↓

D7 — Governance Collaboration

&nbsp; ↓

D8 — Governance Operational Control

&nbsp; ↓

D9 — Governance Operating Improvement

&nbsp; ↓

D10 — Governance Operating Success

D6 tidak menggantikan D5.

D5 menentukan bagaimana review dilakukan.

D6 menentukan bagaimana review dan governance operations disinkronkan  
dalam waktu.

⸻

**3\. Scope Gate**

D6 mencakup temporal coordination untuk:

- governance review;
- decision review;
- compliance activity;
- governance assessment;
- governance coordination;
- governance follow-up;
- governance reporting;
- governance improvement feedback.

D6 tidak menetapkan:

- governance authority;
- decision authority;
- organizational structure;
- policy substance;
- control implementation;
- certification implementation;
- KPI;
- performance targets;
- technical execution.

⸻

**4\. Fundamental Rhythm Principle**

Governance rhythm exists to synchronize governance work; it does not  
create new authority or ownership.

Dengan demikian:

Rhythm

&nbsp; ≠

Authority

Rhythm

&nbsp; ≠

Ownership

Rhythm

&nbsp; ≠

Decision Right

Jadwal atau cadence tidak dapat menciptakan authority yang tidak diberikan  
oleh Part C.

⸻

**5\. Governance Rhythm Architecture**

D6 menggunakan layered temporal model:

Strategic / Governance Direction

&nbsp; ↓

Annual Governance Cycle

&nbsp; ↓

Quarterly Governance Cycle

&nbsp; ↓

Monthly Governance Cycle

&nbsp; ↓

Weekly Governance Cycle

&nbsp; ↓

Daily / Event-Driven Governance Operations

Struktur multi-cycle ini berasal dari reference operating-rhythm model yang  
menempatkan daily, weekly, monthly, quarterly, dan annual cycle sebagai  
lapisan temporal architecture.

Divisi yang Akan Dibangun.txt

Namun, cadence spesifik PD-03 tidak dibekukan dari reference tersebut.

⸻

**6\. Annual Governance Rhythm**

Annual cycle digunakan untuk aktivitas governance yang membutuhkan  
perspektif tahunan.

Secara konseptual dapat mencakup:

- governance operating review;
- governance framework review;
- annual governance priorities;
- governance maturity assessment;
- annual improvement direction;
- annual governance planning.

Exact annual events PD-03:

SOURCE GAP — NOT FROZEN.

Claude Code harus mencari authoritative PD-03 schedule sebelum menetapkan  
event atau tanggal tertentu.

⸻

**7\. Quarterly Governance Rhythm**

Quarterly cycle digunakan untuk governance matters yang membutuhkan  
periodic strategic assessment.

Potential scope:

Quarterly Governance Review

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Findings

&nbsp; ↓

Risk / Compliance Position

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement Priorities

Quarterly rhythm tidak otomatis berarti PD-03 wajib mengadakan meeting  
quarterly.

Frekuensi formal harus berasal dari applicable authority atau operating  
schedule.

⸻

**8\. Monthly Governance Rhythm**

Monthly cycle dapat digunakan untuk:

- governance review;
- unresolved finding review;
- compliance status;
- decision follow-up;
- governance action tracking;
- cross-platform governance coordination.

Namun:

Exact monthly cadence is not frozen as a PD-03 canonical requirement.

⸻

**9\. Weekly Governance Rhythm**

Weekly cycle dapat digunakan untuk operational synchronization ketika  
governance work memiliki active matters.

Contoh concern:

- open governance decisions;
- active reviews;
- compliance actions;
- escalations;
- dependencies;
- evidence requests.

Weekly rhythm bersifat operational pattern, bukan mandatory PD-03 meeting  
schedule.

⸻

**10\. Daily / Event-Driven Rhythm**

Tidak semua governance work mengikuti kalender.

Material governance events dapat memicu activity di luar regular cycle.

Trigger dapat berupa:

- material governance issue;
- urgent compliance matter;
- authority conflict;
- escalation;
- material decision;
- critical finding;
- external requirement;
- significant change.

Model:

Regular Rhythm

&nbsp; +

Event Trigger

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Action

Event-driven operation tidak boleh digunakan untuk bypass governance  
requirements.

⸻

**11\. Rhythm Selection Principle**

Tidak semua governance activity harus mengikuti semua temporal layers.

Selection harus mempertimbangkan:

- materiality;
- urgency;
- governance impact;
- compliance impact;
- cross-platform impact;
- authority requirement;
- review requirement;
- dependency;
- existing governance schedule.

Model:

Materiality

&nbsp; ↓

Required Rhythm

&nbsp; ↓

Review Depth

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence Requirement

**23\. Rhythm Synchronization**

Berbagai governance cycles harus disinkronkan agar tidak menciptakan

Compliance Cycle

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ├──── Governance Review

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ├──── Decision Review

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ├──── Executive Interface

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; └──── Improvement Cycle

Jika dua review memiliki overlap material, operating owner harus  
mengonsolidasikan evidence apabila governance requirements mengizinkan.

**24\. Avoiding Review Duplication**

D6 harus mencegah:

- evidence dikumpulkan berulang tanpa alasan;
- review yang sama dijalankan dua kali;
- conflicting cadence;
- duplicate reporting;
- duplicate finding registers.

Namun consolidation tidak boleh menghilangkan requirement yang diwajibkan  
oleh authority berbeda.

Operating rhythm memiliki owner yang bertanggung jawab memastikan cycle

&nbsp; ≠

Decision Authority

seluruh governance decision.

SOURCE GAP — NOT FROZEN.

- action deadlines;
- escalation timing;
- validation timing.

Calendar tidak boleh menjadi sumber authority baru.

Perubahan rhythm harus mempertimbangkan:

- affected governance activity;
- dependency;
- authority;
- downstream review;
- compliance requirement;
- affected Platforms.

Material rhythm change harus traceable.

Operating convenience saja tidak cukup untuk membatalkan mandatory  
governance requirement.

- cadence effectiveness;
