**64\. Canonical Freeze Statement**

D9 — Governance Operating Improvement establishes the Operating Improvement Constitution through which PD-03 Governance & Compliance continuously develops its operating model using operational evidence, findings, learning, innovation, validation, and controlled change while preserving governance authority, organizational ownership, accountability, compliance boundaries, and Platform autonomy.

D9 is the improvement layer between D8 Operational Control and D10 Operating Success. D8 provides operational evidence; D9 converts evidence into controlled improvement; D10 evaluates the resulting operating condition.

Improvement does not create authority, transfer organizational ownership, replace governance, replace operational control, replace performance architecture, or authorize organizational restructuring. Material changes must use the authority applicable to the type of change.

For cross-platform improvement, PD-03 may identify governance or compliance improvement requirements and validate evidence, while the applicable Platform Division retains ownership of domain execution and implementation.

Exact PD-03 improvement taxonomy, priority model, thresholds, SLA, approval matrix, status taxonomy, repository schema, scoring, cadence, experimentation policy, rollback policy, and lessons-learned schema remain SOURCE GAP until verified against authoritative PD-03 source.

D9 is therefore FROZEN as the Governance Operating Improvement Constitution for PD-03 Governance & Compliance, with all unsupported implementation details explicitly reserved for Claude Code source reconciliation.

\### Freeze verdict

1\. Purpose

D10 menetapkan Operating Success Constitution bagi PD-03 Governance &  
Compliance.

D10 menjawab pertanyaan:

Kapan Operating Model PD-03 dianggap berhasil dalam menjalankan mandat Governance & Compliance secara efektif, efisien, controlled, traceable, sustainable, dan continuously improving?

D10 bukan sekadar daftar success metrics.

D10 menetapkan konstitusi keberhasilan operasi yang menjadi dasar untuk  
menilai apakah keseluruhan operating architecture D1–D9 telah menghasilkan  
kondisi operasi yang diharapkan.

⸻

6\. Operating Success Purpose

D10 memiliki tujuan untuk memastikan bahwa PD-03:

0\. menjalankan operating mandate secara konsisten;

0\. menghasilkan governance & compliance work yang dapat ditelusuri;

0\. menjalankan governance mechanism tanpa material operational breakdown;

0\. menjaga control integrity;

0\. menjaga decision dan review traceability;

0\. berkoordinasi dengan Platform lain tanpa mengambil ownership mereka;

0\. mampu merespons governance dan compliance needs secara proporsional;

0\. mampu memperbaiki operating weakness berdasarkan evidence;

0\. mempertahankan operating sustainability;

0\. menyediakan evidence yang cukup bagi Performance Architecture.

⸻

7\. Operating Success Philosophy

OSP-01 — Success Means Reliable Operation

Operating model dianggap berhasil apabila dapat menjalankan fungsi yang  
dimandatkan secara reliable.

Success bukan sekadar:

• aktivitas selesai;

• meeting terlaksana;

• dokumen diproduksi.

Success harus terlihat pada quality of operation.

⸻

OSP-02 — Success Requires Boundary Integrity

Operating model tidak dianggap berhasil apabila keberhasilan dicapai dengan  
melanggar:

• authority boundary;

• organizational boundary;

• governance boundary;

• Platform autonomy;

• compliance requirement.

⸻

OSP-03 — Success Requires Traceability

Material governance operation harus dapat ditelusuri dari:

Need

&nbsp;↓

Work

&nbsp;↓

Decision

&nbsp;↓

Review

&nbsp;↓

Artifact

&nbsp;↓

Outcome

⸻

OSP-04 — Success Requires Control Integrity

Operating efficiency tidak boleh dicapai dengan menghilangkan required  
governance control.

⸻

OSP-05 — Success Includes Adaptability

Operating model harus mampu belajar dan memperbaiki diri ketika evidence  
menunjukkan adanya weakness.

⸻

OSP-06 — Success Must Be Sustainable

Operating model tidak dianggap berhasil hanya karena menghasilkan outcome  
jangka pendek dengan biaya operational atau governance burden yang tidak  
sustainable.

⸻

⸻

9\. Effectiveness

Effectiveness menjawab:

Apakah PD-03 mampu menghasilkan intended governance & compliance operating  
outcomes?

Indikator konseptual effectiveness meliputi:

• mandate execution;

• required governance coverage;

• decision support quality;

• review completion;

• control operation;

• compliance support;

• closure of material governance work;

• quality of governance outputs.

Effectiveness tidak sama dengan jumlah aktivitas.

⸻

10\. Efficiency

Efficiency menjawab:

Apakah intended governance outcome dicapai dengan penggunaan operating  
effort yang proporsional?

PD-03 harus menghindari:

• duplicated review;

• unnecessary escalation;

• redundant governance work;

• excessive coordination;

• unnecessary approval loops;

• avoidable documentation burden.

Efficiency tidak boleh mengorbankan control integrity.

⸻

11\. Integrity

Operating integrity berarti operasi:

• tetap berada dalam mandate;

• menggunakan authority yang benar;

• mempertahankan ownership;

• menjaga segregation of concern;

• tidak melakukan unauthorized boundary expansion;

• tidak mengubah historical evidence;

• tidak bypass governance control.

Model:

Operational Success

&nbsp; ↓

Must Preserve

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Integrity

12\. Traceability

Operating success membutuhkan traceability.

Minimum chain:

Governance Need

&nbsp; ↓

Operating Work

&nbsp; ↓

&nbsp; ↓

Decision / Review

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Artifact

&nbsp; ↓

Implementation / Coordination

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Outcome

⸻

13\. Responsiveness

Responsiveness menjawab:

Apakah operating model mampu merespons governance dan compliance needs  
pada waktu yang sesuai dengan materiality-nya?

Responsiveness tidak berarti:

semua request harus diproses secepat mungkin.

Responsiveness harus mempertimbangkan:

• materiality;

• urgency;

• risk;

• dependency;

• authority;

• evidence readiness.

Exact SLA atau threshold tidak dibekukan karena belum terdapat authoritative  
PD-03 source yang menetapkannya.

⸻

14\. Adaptability

Operating model harus mampu beradaptasi terhadap:

• regulatory change;

• governance change;

• organizational change;

• recurring operational weakness;

• lessons learned;

• emerging compliance need;

• cross-platform requirement.

Adaptability harus dilakukan melalui D9 improvement mechanism dan applicable  
change authority.

⸻

15\. Sustainability

Operating success harus sustainable.

Operating model tidak dianggap successful apabila:

• bergantung pada extraordinary effort secara terus-menerus;

• memiliki recurring unresolved breakdown;

• memiliki excessive coordination burden;

• membutuhkan manual workaround permanen;

• menghasilkan governance debt yang terus meningkat.

Sustainability menjadi indikator apakah operating model benar-benar sehat,  
bukan hanya mampu menghasilkan output sementara.

⸻

• control improvement dapat dilakukan.

26\. Operating Success Criteria

PD-03 operating model memenuhi success condition apabila:

OSC-01 — Mandate Alignment

Operating activity tetap aligned dengan PD-03 mandate.

OSC-02 — Boundary Integrity

Tidak terdapat material unauthorized boundary expansion.

OSC-03 — Ownership Clarity

Primary operational ownership dapat ditentukan.

OSC-04 — Governance Integrity

Required governance mechanism tidak dibypass.

OSC-05 — Traceability

Material governance work dapat ditelusuri.

OSC-06 — Decision Integrity

Decision flow menggunakan applicable authority.

OSC-07 — Control Integrity

Required operational control tetap effective.

OSC-08 — Coordination Quality

Cross-platform coordination tidak menghasilkan ownership ambiguity.

OSC-09 — Responsiveness

Operating model mampu merespons kebutuhan secara proportional terhadap  
materiality dan urgency.

OSC-10 — Improvement Capability

Operating weakness dapat diubah menjadi improvement opportunity.

OSC-11 — Learning

Lessons learned dapat masuk kembali ke operating model.

OSC-12 — Sustainability

Operating model dapat dipertahankan tanpa recurring structural breakdown.

⸻

• reduction of recurring weakness.

31\. Operational Maturity

D10 menggunakan maturity sebagai directional assessment, bukan fixed  
numerical model.

Canonical direction:

Reactive

&nbsp; ↓

Defined

&nbsp; ↓

Managed

&nbsp; ↓

Controlled

&nbsp; ↓

Adaptive

Reactive

Operating model terutama merespons issue setelah terjadi.

Defined

Operating mechanism mulai terdokumentasi dan konsisten.

Managed

Operation memiliki control, ownership, review, dan evidence.

Controlled

Operating performance, integrity, improvement, dan risk dapat dikelola  
secara sistematis.

Adaptive

Operating model mampu belajar dan menyesuaikan diri tanpa kehilangan  
constitutional integrity.

Exact scoring, threshold, dan maturity gate tetap SOURCE GAP sampai  
ditetapkan oleh authoritative PD-03 source.

44\. Operating Success Statement

Canonical statement:

PD-03 Governance & Compliance Operating Model is successful when it reliably executes its Governance & Compliance mandate through effective, efficient, controlled, traceable, responsive, adaptive, and sustainable operations while preserving governance authority, organizational ownership, compliance integrity, historical evidence, and the autonomy of other AIOS Platform Divisions.

Versi Bahasa Indonesia:

Operating Model PD-03 Governance & Compliance dinyatakan berhasil apabila mampu menjalankan mandat Governance & Compliance secara reliable melalui operasi yang efektif, efisien, controlled, traceable, responsive, adaptive, dan sustainable, dengan tetap menjaga authority governance, organizational ownership, compliance integrity, historical evidence, serta autonomy Platform AIOS lainnya.

51\. Canonical Freeze Statement

D10 — Governance Operating Success establishes the Operating Success Constitution for PD-03 Governance & Compliance. It defines the conditions under which the Governance & Compliance Operating Model is considered successful, using effectiveness, efficiency, integrity, traceability, responsiveness, adaptability, and sustainability as the constitutional dimensions of operating success.

D10 evaluates the operating model; it does not replace C10 Governance Success, Part E Performance Architecture, organizational authority, or lifecycle authority.

PD-03 operating success must be achieved while preserving its Governance & Compliance boundary, including Policy, Control, Certification, and Compliance responsibilities, without assuming technical-design ownership or execution ownership of other Platform Divisions.

D9 provides the improvement mechanism; D10 determines whether the resulting operating condition is successful. Success is continuously validated through evidence and may trigger further improvement.

Numerical targets, scoring, SLA, maturity thresholds, cadence, status taxonomy, and exact assessment schema remain SOURCE GAP until verified against authoritative PD-03 source.

D10 is therefore FROZEN as the Governance Operating Success Constitution for Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance, with implementation-level details explicitly reserved for source reconciliation.

**6\. Operating Success Purpose**

D10 memiliki tujuan untuk memastikan bahwa PD-03:

1. menjalankan operating mandate secara konsisten;
2. menghasilkan governance & compliance work yang dapat ditelusuri;
3. menjalankan governance mechanism tanpa material operational breakdown;
4. menjaga control integrity;
5. menjaga decision dan review traceability;
6. berkoordinasi dengan Platform lain tanpa mengambil ownership mereka;
7. mampu merespons governance dan compliance needs secara proporsional;
8. mampu memperbaiki operating weakness berdasarkan evidence;
9. mempertahankan operating sustainability;
10. menyediakan evidence yang cukup bagi Performance Architecture.

⸻

**7\. Operating Success Philosophy**

**OSP-01 — Success Means Reliable Operation**

Operating model dianggap berhasil apabila dapat menjalankan fungsi yang  
dimandatkan secara reliable.

Success bukan sekadar:

- aktivitas selesai;
- meeting terlaksana;
- dokumen diproduksi.

Success harus terlihat pada quality of operation.

⸻

**OSP-02 — Success Requires Boundary Integrity**

Operating model tidak dianggap berhasil apabila keberhasilan dicapai dengan  
melanggar:

- authority boundary;
- organizational boundary;
- governance boundary;
- Platform autonomy;
- compliance requirement.

⸻

**OSP-03 — Success Requires Traceability**

Material governance operation harus dapat ditelusuri dari:

Need

&nbsp;↓

Work

&nbsp;↓

Decision

&nbsp;↓

Review

&nbsp;↓

Artifact

&nbsp;↓

Outcome

⸻

**OSP-04 — Success Requires Control Integrity**

Operating efficiency tidak boleh dicapai dengan menghilangkan required  
governance control.

⸻

**OSP-05 — Success Includes Adaptability**

Operating model harus mampu belajar dan memperbaiki diri ketika evidence  
menunjukkan adanya weakness.

⸻

**OSP-06 — Success Must Be Sustainable**

Operating model tidak dianggap berhasil hanya karena menghasilkan outcome  
jangka pendek dengan biaya operational atau governance burden yang tidak  
sustainable.

⸻

**8\. Operating Success Dimensions**

D10 menggunakan dimensi keberhasilan berikut sebagai constitutional model:

&nbsp; GOVERNANCE OPERATING SUCCESS

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ┌───────────────────┼───────────────────┐

Dimensi tersebut digunakan untuk mengevaluasi operating condition, bukan  
sebagai fixed numerical score.

⸻

**9\. Effectiveness**

Effectiveness menjawab:

Apakah PD-03 mampu menghasilkan intended governance & compliance operating  
outcomes?

Indikator konseptual effectiveness meliputi:

- mandate execution;
- required governance coverage;
- decision support quality;
- review completion;
- control operation;
- compliance support;
- closure of material governance work;
- quality of governance outputs.

Effectiveness tidak sama dengan jumlah aktivitas.

⸻

**10\. Efficiency**

Efficiency menjawab:

Apakah intended governance outcome dicapai dengan penggunaan operating  
effort yang proporsional?

PD-03 harus menghindari:

- duplicated review;
- unnecessary escalation;
- redundant governance work;
- excessive coordination;
- unnecessary approval loops;
- avoidable documentation burden.

Efficiency tidak boleh mengorbankan control integrity.

⸻

**11\. Integrity**

Operating integrity berarti operasi:

- tetap berada dalam mandate;
- menggunakan authority yang benar;
- mempertahankan ownership;
- menjaga segregation of concern;
- tidak melakukan unauthorized boundary expansion;
- tidak mengubah historical evidence;
- tidak bypass governance control.

Model:

Operational Success

&nbsp; ↓

Must Preserve

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Integrity

⸻

**12\. Traceability**

Operating success membutuhkan traceability.

Minimum chain:

Governance Need

&nbsp; ↓

Operating Work

&nbsp; ↓

Responsible Owner

&nbsp; ↓

Decision / Review

&nbsp; ↓

Governance Artifact

&nbsp; ↓

Implementation / Coordination

&nbsp; ↓

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Outcome

Jika material work tidak dapat ditelusuri, operating success tidak dapat  
dianggap fully established.

⸻

**13\. Responsiveness**

Responsiveness menjawab:

Apakah operating model mampu merespons governance dan compliance needs  
pada waktu yang sesuai dengan materiality-nya?

Responsiveness tidak berarti:

semua request harus diproses secepat mungkin.

Responsiveness harus mempertimbangkan:

- materiality;
- urgency;
- risk;
- dependency;
- authority;
- evidence readiness.

Exact SLA atau threshold tidak dibekukan karena belum terdapat authoritative  
PD-03 source yang menetapkannya.

⸻

**14\. Adaptability**

Operating model harus mampu beradaptasi terhadap:

- regulatory change;
- governance change;
- organizational change;
- recurring operational weakness;
- lessons learned;
- emerging compliance need;
- cross-platform requirement.

Adaptability harus dilakukan melalui D9 improvement mechanism dan applicable  
change authority.

⸻

**15\. Sustainability**

Operating success harus sustainable.

Operating model tidak dianggap successful apabila:

- bergantung pada extraordinary effort secara terus-menerus;
- memiliki recurring unresolved breakdown;
- memiliki excessive coordination burden;
- membutuhkan manual workaround permanen;
- menghasilkan governance debt yang terus meningkat.

Sustainability menjadi indikator apakah operating model benar-benar sehat,  
bukan hanya mampu menghasilkan output sementara.

⸻

**16\. PD-03 Operating Success Domain**

Operating success PD-03 harus tetap berakar pada domain:

Governance & Compliance

&nbsp; │

&nbsp; ├── Policy

&nbsp; ├── Control

&nbsp; ├── Certification

&nbsp; └── Compliance

Baseline PD-03 menempatkan Governance & Compliance sebagai domain utama dan  
memberikan responsibility Policy, Control, Certification, serta Compliance.

Performance Architecture Review.txt

D10 tidak boleh memperluas domain tersebut menjadi general enterprise  
execution ownership.

⸻

**17\. Policy Operating Success**

Policy operation dianggap successful apabila:

- governance policy dapat dikelola secara controlled;
- policy lifecycle dapat ditelusuri;
- policy decisions memiliki applicable authority;
- policy artifacts konsisten;
- policy-related work dapat direview;
- policy change dapat dikendalikan.

D10 tidak menetapkan isi policy.

⸻

**18\. Control Operating Success**

Control operation dianggap successful apabila:

- governance controls dapat dijalankan;
- control ownership jelas;
- control evidence tersedia;
- exceptions dapat ditangani;
- control weakness dapat diidentifikasi;
- control improvement dapat dilakukan.

PD-03 tidak otomatis mengambil alih technical control implementation dari  
Platform lain.

Governance Control

&nbsp; ↓

PD-03

Control Execution

- evidence dapat ditelusuri;
- review dapat dilakukan;
- findings dapat dicatat;
- certification outcome dapat ditelusuri;
- closure dapat diverifikasi.

Certification tidak sama dengan implementation ownership.

Certification

&nbsp; ≠

Execution

⸻

**20\. Compliance Operating Success**

Compliance operation dianggap successful apabila:

- compliance obligations dapat diidentifikasi;
- applicable ownership jelas;
- compliance evidence dapat ditelusuri;
- findings dapat ditangani;
- escalation berjalan apabila diperlukan;
- compliance status dapat dievaluasi.

PD-03 tidak otomatis menjadi execution owner atas compliance action pada  
Platform lain.

⸻

**21\. Governance Coordination Success**

Cross-platform governance operation dianggap successful apabila:

- governance requirement dapat dikomunikasikan;
- applicable owner dapat diidentifikasi;
- dependency dapat ditelusuri;
- decision authority tetap jelas;
- Platform autonomy tetap dipertahankan;
- evidence dapat dikembalikan ke governance process.

Applicable Platform

&nbsp; ↓

Platform Execution

Evidence

**22\. Decision Operating Success**

Decision operation dianggap successful apabila:

- decision request memiliki context;
- evidence tersedia secara proporsional;
- authority dapat diidentifikasi;
- decision review terjadi apabila diperlukan;
- decision outcome terdokumentasi;
- decision dapat ditelusuri ke implementation atau follow-up.

D10 tidak menciptakan decision authority baru.

⸻

**23\. Review Operating Success**

Governance review operation dianggap successful apabila:

- review memiliki scope;
- reviewer/authority sesuai;
- evidence tersedia;
- finding dapat ditelusuri;
- disposition jelas;
- unresolved issue memiliki path yang sesuai.

Review completion saja tidak cukup.

Review harus menghasilkan usable governance evidence.

**26\. Operating Success Criteria**

PD-03 operating model memenuhi success condition apabila:

OSC-01 — Mandate Alignment

Operating activity tetap aligned dengan PD-03 mandate.

OSC-02 — Boundary Integrity

Tidak terdapat material unauthorized boundary expansion.

OSC-03 — Ownership Clarity

Primary operational ownership dapat ditentukan.

OSC-04 — Governance Integrity

Required governance mechanism tidak dibypass.

OSC-05 — Traceability

Material governance work dapat ditelusuri.

OSC-06 — Decision Integrity

Decision flow menggunakan applicable authority.

OSC-07 — Control Integrity

Required operational control tetap effective.

OSC-08 — Coordination Quality

Cross-platform coordination tidak menghasilkan ownership ambiguity.

OSC-09 — Responsiveness

Operating model mampu merespons kebutuhan secara proportional terhadap  
materiality dan urgency.

OSC-10 — Improvement Capability

Operating weakness dapat diubah menjadi improvement opportunity.

OSC-11 — Learning

Lessons learned dapat masuk kembali ke operating model.

OSC-12 — Sustainability

Operating model dapat dipertahankan tanpa recurring structural breakdown.

⸻

**27\. Operational Effectiveness Indicators**

Indikator effectiveness dapat mencakup:

- completion of intended governance work;
- closure of material findings;
- quality of governance outputs;
- successful review outcomes;
- governance decision readiness;
- certification completion;
- compliance support effectiveness.

Exact metric definition dan target harus ditetapkan melalui Performance  
Architecture apabila diperlukan.

⸻

**28\. Operational Efficiency Indicators**

Indikator efficiency dapat mencakup:

- duplicated work;
- unnecessary review cycles;
- avoidable escalation;
- coordination overhead;
- rework;
- unresolved handoff;
- documentation burden.

D10 tidak menetapkan numerical target.

⸻

**29\. Operational Integrity Indicators**

Indikator integrity dapat mencakup:

- unauthorized process deviation;
- governance bypass;
- ownership ambiguity;
- untraceable decision;
- missing evidence;
- control degradation;
- historical evidence mutation.

Material integrity failure dapat menghalangi success declaration meskipun  
operational output tinggi.

⸻

**30\. Operational Adaptability Indicators**

Indikator adaptability dapat mencakup:

- response to governance change;
- improvement cycle completion;
- lessons-learned adoption;
- ability to absorb new compliance requirements;
- operating model refinement;
- reduction of recurring weakness.

⸻

**31\. Operational Maturity**

D10 menggunakan maturity sebagai directional assessment, bukan fixed  
numerical model.

Canonical direction:

Reactive

&nbsp; ↓

Defined

&nbsp; ↓

Managed

&nbsp; ↓

Controlled

&nbsp; ↓

Adaptive

Reactive

Operating model terutama merespons issue setelah terjadi.

Defined

Operating mechanism mulai terdokumentasi dan konsisten.

Managed

Operation memiliki control, ownership, review, dan evidence.

Controlled

Operating performance, integrity, improvement, dan risk dapat dikelola  
secara sistematis.

Adaptive

Operating model mampu belajar dan menyesuaikan diri tanpa kehilangan  
constitutional integrity.

Exact scoring, threshold, dan maturity gate tetap SOURCE GAP sampai  
ditetapkan oleh authoritative PD-03 source.

⸻

**32\. Success Assessment Model**

Assessment mengikuti:

Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Assessment

&nbsp; ↓

Dimension Review

&nbsp; ↓

Boundary Check

&nbsp; ↓

Success Determination

&nbsp; ↓

Improvement / Maintain

Assessment harus menggunakan evidence yang tersedia.

Tidak boleh menetapkan success hanya berdasarkan subjective declaration.

⸻

**33\. Success Assessment Questions**

Assessment harus menjawab:

1. Apakah operating model menjalankan mandate?
2. Apakah ownership tetap jelas?
3. Apakah governance boundary dipertahankan?
4. Apakah material work traceable?
5. Apakah decision flow berjalan melalui applicable authority?
6. Apakah controls berfungsi?
7. Apakah coordination berjalan tanpa ownership takeover?
8. Apakah operating friction dapat ditangani?
9. Apakah improvement menghasilkan improvement nyata?
10. Apakah operating model sustainable?

⸻

**34\. Material Failure Condition**

Operating success tidak boleh dinyatakan fully achieved apabila terdapat  
material:

- authority violation;
- ownership violation;
- governance bypass;
- control failure;
- untraceable material decision;
- persistent unresolved operational breakdown;
- unauthorized cross-platform execution takeover.

Materiality harus ditentukan melalui applicable governance/performance  
mechanism; D10 tidak mengarang threshold numerik.

⸻

**35\. Success Review Cycle**

D10 menjadi reference point untuk review operating success.

Review dapat menggunakan:

Operating Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

D8 Control Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

D9 Improvement Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

Performance Evidence

&nbsp; ↓

D10 Success Assessment

Exact review cadence tidak dibekukan pada D10 karena cadence merupakan

**44\. Operating Success Statement**

Canonical statement:

PD-03 Governance & Compliance Operating Model is successful when it reliably executes its Governance & Compliance mandate through effective, efficient, controlled, traceable, responsive, adaptive, and sustainable operations while preserving governance authority, organizational ownership, compliance integrity, historical evidence, and the autonomy of other AIOS Platform Divisions.

Versi Bahasa Indonesia:

Operating Model PD-03 Governance & Compliance dinyatakan berhasil apabila mampu menjalankan mandat Governance & Compliance secara reliable melalui operasi yang efektif, efisien, controlled, traceable, responsive, adaptive, dan sustainable, dengan tetap menjaga authority governance, organizational ownership, compliance integrity, historical evidence, serta autonomy Platform AIOS lainnya.

**51\. Canonical Freeze Statement**

D10 — Governance Operating Success establishes the Operating Success Constitution for PD-03 Governance & Compliance. It defines the conditions under which the Governance & Compliance Operating Model is considered successful, using effectiveness, efficiency, integrity, traceability, responsiveness, adaptability, and sustainability as the constitutional dimensions of operating success.

D10 evaluates the operating model; it does not replace C10 Governance Success, Part E Performance Architecture, organizational authority, or lifecycle authority.

PD-03 operating success must be achieved while preserving its Governance & Compliance boundary, including Policy, Control, Certification, and Compliance responsibilities, without assuming technical-design ownership or execution ownership of other Platform Divisions.

D9 provides the improvement mechanism; D10 determines whether the resulting operating condition is successful. Success is continuously validated through evidence and may trigger further improvement.

Numerical targets, scoring, SLA, maturity thresholds, cadence, status taxonomy, and exact assessment schema remain SOURCE GAP until verified against authoritative PD-03 source.

D10 is therefore FROZEN as the Governance Operating Success Constitution for Volume 3 — PD-03 Governance & Compliance, with implementation-level details explicitly reserved for source reconciliation.

\### Final Freeze Verdict

\*\*D10 — Governance Operating Success: \`FROZEN — SOURCE-BOUNDED\`.\*\*
